Implement the following plan:

# Plan: Integrate Langfuse with Claude Agent SDK via OpenTelemetry Auto-Instrumentation

## Context

The current Langfuse integration uses ~100 lines of manual span/generation tracking in `agent.py` — manually parsing streaming events to create generation spans, tracking accumulated thinking/answer text, and managing tool spans. This approach is complex and bug-prone (e.g., we just fixed a `has_thinking` flag not resetting between turns). The official [Langfuse + Claude Agent SDK guide](https://langfuse.com/integrations/frameworks/claude-agent-sdk) recommends OpenTelemetry auto-instrumentation via `langsmith[claude-agent-sdk]`, which automatically captures LLM calls, tool calls, and token usage — eliminating all manual tracking.

**Key finding:** `@observe()` has a known issue with FastAPI `StreamingResponse` and async generators ([GitHub #8216](https://github.com/langfuse/langfuse/issues/8216)). We'll use `start_as_current_observation()` with manual `__enter__`/`__exit__` instead.

## Changes

### 1. Add langsmith dependency (`backend/pyproject.toml`)

Add to `[tool.poetry.dependencies]`:
```toml
langsmith = {extras = ["claude-agent-sdk", "otel"], version = ">=0.3.0"}
```

Then run `poetry lock && poetry install`.

### 2. Add OTel env vars (`backend/.env.example`)

Append:
```
LANGSMITH_OTEL_ENABLED=true
LANGSMITH_OTEL_ONLY=true
LANGSMITH_TRACING=true
```

### 3. Rewrite tracing module (`backend/app/tracing.py`)

- Use `langfuse.get_client()` (singleton, reads from env vars) instead of `Langfuse()` constructor
- Call `configure_claude_agent_sdk()` from `langsmith.integrations.claude_agent_sdk` at startup
- Set OTel env vars programmatically via `os.environ.setdefault()`
- Keep `get_langfuse_client()` and `get_langfuse_dashboard_url()` APIs unchanged
- Remove direct imports of `LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY`, `LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY`, `LANGFUSE_BASE_URL` (not needed with `get_client()`)

### 4. Simplify agent tracing (`backend/app/agent.py`)

**Remove (~100 lines):**
- `_truncate()` helper
- All tracing state: `current_generation`, `turn_number`, `accumulated_thinking`, `accumulated_answer`
- `_start_generation()` and `_end_generation()` helper functions
- Manual generation span tracking in `content_block_start`/`content_block_delta` handlers
- Manual tool span creation in `ToolUseBlock` handler
- Trace finalization in `finally` block (`_end_generation()`, `trace.update_trace()`, `trace.end()`)

**Add (~15 lines):**
- Import `propagate_attributes` from `langfuse`
- `start_as_current_observation()` context (manual `__enter__`/`__exit__` for async generator compatibility)
- `propagate_attributes()` for session_id and metadata
- `langfuse.update_current_trace()` for trace output
- `langfuse.flush()` in finally block

**Keep unchanged:**
- `_extract_tool_result_text()` (still needed for SSE)
- `build_system_prompt()`
- All SSE streaming logic (thinking/answer/tool_call/tool_result/done events)
- SQL execution for frontend display (auto-instrumentation captures MCP execution separately)

### 5. Update design doc (`docs/plans/2026-02-20-langfuse-integration-design.md`)

Update "Approach" from manual spans to OTel auto-instrumentation. Update backend section to reflect simplified tracing. Add langsmith dependency info.

### 6. Update implementation doc (`docs/plans/2026-02-20-langfuse-integration-plan.md`)

Update tasks to reflect new approach — simplified tracing module, simplified agent code.

## Files Modified

| File | Change |
|---|---|
| `backend/pyproject.toml` | Add `langsmith[claude-agent-sdk,otel]` |
| `backend/.env.example` | Add 3 OTel env vars |
| `backend/app/tracing.py` | Rewrite: `get_client()` + `configure_claude_agent_sdk()` |
| `backend/app/agent.py` | Remove ~100 lines manual tracing, add ~15 lines OTel context |
| `docs/plans/2026-02-20-langfuse-integration-design.md` | Update approach |
| `docs/plans/2026-02-20-langfuse-integration-plan.md` | Update tasks |

## Verification

1. **Without Langfuse configured**: Backend starts, agent chat works, no tracing overhead
2. **With Langfuse configured**: Traces appear in Langfuse dashboard with auto-captured LLM generations, tool calls, and token usage nested under the `agent-chat` trace
3. **Frontend**: Langfuse Traces button works as before (status endpoint unchanged)

    15→def _init_langfuse():

    18→    if not LANGFUSE_ENABLED:
    19→        logger.info("Langfuse not configured, tracing disabled")
    20→        return
    21→
    22→    try:
    23→        from langfuse import Langfuse
    24→
    25→        _langfuse_client = Langfuse(
    26→            REDACTED,
    27→            secret_key=LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY,
    28→            base_url=LANGFUSE_BASE_URL,
    29→        )
    30→        if _langfuse_client.auth_check():
    31→            logger.info("Langfuse connected successfully")
    32→        else:
    33→            logger.warning("Langfuse auth check failed, tracing disabled")
    34→            _langfuse_client = None
    35→    except Exception as e:
    36→        logger.warning("Failed to initialize Langfuse: %s", e)
    37→        _langfuse_client = None
    38→

    40→def get_langfuse_client():
    41→    """Return the Langfuse client or None if not configured."""
    42→    return _langfuse_client
    43→
    44→
    45→def get_langfuse_dashboard_url() -> str | None:
    46→    """Return the Langfuse project traces URL or None."""
    47→    if _langfuse_client is None:
    48→        return None
    49→    try:
    50→        project_id = _langfuse_client._get_project_id()
    51→        if project_id:
    52→            return f"{LANGFUSE_BASE_URL}/project/{project_id}/traces"
    53→    except Exception as e:
    54→        logger.warning("Failed to get Langfuse project ID: %s", e)
    55→    return LANGFUSE_BASE_URL
    56→
    57→
    58→# Initialize on module import
    59→_init_langfuse()
    60→

     1→import json
     2→import logging
     3→from typing import AsyncIterator

    10→    ResultMessage,
    11→    ToolUseBlock,
    12→    ToolResultBlock,

    14→from claude_agent_sdk._errors import MessageParseError
    15→from claude_agent_sdk._internal.message_parser import parse_message
    16→from claude_agent_sdk.types import StreamEvent
    17→from app.tools import create_duckdb_server
    18→from app.database import db

    22→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

    24→
    25→def build_system_prompt() -> str:
    26→    tables = db.list_tables()
    27→    prompt = """You are a helpful data analyst assistant working with a DuckDB database.
    28→You can execute SQL queries using the execute_sql tool to answer questions about the user's data.
    29→
    30→Guidelines:
    31→- Write clear, efficient DuckDB SQL queries
    32→- When exploring data, start with small queries (use LIMIT)
    33→- Explain your findings in plain language after getting results
    34→- If a query fails, try to fix it and retry
    35→- Use double quotes for table and column names that might conflict with reserved words
    36→"""
    37→    if not tables:
    38→        prompt += "\nNo tables are currently loaded. Ask the user to upload a CSV file first."
    39→    else:
    40→        prompt += "\nCurrently loaded tables:\n"
    41→        for table in tables:
    42→            prompt += f'\nTable: "{table["name"]}" ({table["rowCount"]} rows)\nColumns:\n'
    43→            for col in table["columns"]:
    44→                prompt += f'  - "{col["name"]}" ({col["type"]})\n'
    45→    return prompt
    46→
    47→
    48→def _truncate(text: str, max_len: int = 1000) -> str:
    49→    """Truncate text for trace data, appending '...' if trimmed."""
    50→    if len(text) <= max_len:
    51→        return text
    52→    return text[:max_len] + "..."
    53→
    54→
    55→def _extract_tool_result_text(content: object) -> str:
    56→    """Extract text from ToolResultBlock.content."""
    57→    if content is None:
    58→        return ""
    59→    if isinstance(content, str):
    60→        return content
    61→    if isinstance(content, list):
    62→        parts = []
    63→        for item in content:
    64→            if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("type") == "text":
    65→                parts.append(item.get("text", ""))
    66→        return "\n".join(parts)
    67→    return str(content)
    68→
    69→
    70→async def stream_chat(message: str, session_id: str | None = None) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
    71→    """Stream agent chat responses as SSE events."""
    72→    duckdb_server = create_duckdb_server()
    73→
    74→    logger.info("Using model: %s", ANTHROPIC_MODEL)
    75→    options = ClaudeAgentOptions(
    76→        model=ANTHROPIC_MODEL,
    77→        system_prompt=build_system_prompt(),
    78→        mcp_servers={"duckdb": duckdb_server},
    79→        allowed_tools=["mcp__duckdb__execute_sql"],
    80→        permission_mode="bypassPermissions",
    81→        max_turns=20,
    82→        include_partial_messages=True,
    83→    )
    84→
    85→    client = ClaudeSDKClient(options=options)
    86→    actual_session_id = session_id
    87→
    88→    # --- Langfuse tracing setup (conditional) ---
    89→    langfuse = get_langfuse_client()
    90→    trace = None
    91→    if langfuse:
    92→        try:
    93→            trace = langfuse.start_span(name="agent-chat")
    94→            trace.update_trace(
    95→                session_id=session_id or "default",
    96→                input={"message": _truncate(message, 500)},
    97→                metadata={"model": ANTHROPIC_MODEL},
    98→            )
    99→        except Exception as e:
   100→            logger.debug("Failed to create Langfuse trace: %s", e)
   101→            trace = None
   102→
   103→    # Tracing state
   104→    current_generation = None
   105→    turn_number = 0
   106→    accumulated_thinking = ""
   107→    accumulated_answer = ""
   108→
   109→    def _end_generation():
   110→        """End the current generation span, recording accumulated text."""
   111→        nonlocal current_generation, accumulated_thinking, accumulated_answer
   112→        if current_generation:
   113→            try:
   114→                output_data = {}
   115→                if accumulated_thinking:
   116→                    output_data["thinking"] = _truncate(accumulated_thinking)
   117→                if accumulated_answer:
   118→                    output_data["text"] = _truncate(accumulated_answer)
   119→                current_generation.update(output=output_data)
   120→                current_generation.end()
   121→            except Exception as e:
   122→                logger.debug("Failed to end Langfuse generation: %s", e)
   123→            current_generation = None
   124→        accumulated_thinking = ""
   125→        accumulated_answer = ""
   126→
   127→    def _start_generation():
   128→        """Start a new generation span for an LLM turn."""
   129→        nonlocal current_generation, turn_number
   130→        if not trace:
   131→            return
   132→        _end_generation()
   133→        turn_number += 1
   134→        try:
   135→            input_data = (
   136→                {"message": _truncate(message, 500)}
   137→                if turn_number == 1
   138→                else {"continued": True}
   139→            )
   140→            current_generation = trace.start_generation(
   141→                name=f"llm-turn-{turn_number}",
   142→                model=ANTHROPIC_MODEL,
   143→                input=input_data,
   144→            )
   145→        except Exception as e:
   146→            logger.debug("Failed to create Langfuse generation: %s", e)
   147→            current_generation = None
   148→
   149→    try:
   150→        await client.connect()
   151→        await client.query(message, session_id=session_id or "default")
   152→
   153→        current_text = ""
   154→        has_tool_calls = False
   155→        thinking_sent = False
   156→        has_thinking = False
   157→        sql_result_ids: set[str] = set()
   158→        tool_names: dict[str, str] = {}
   159→
   160→        async for raw_data in client._query.receive_messages():
   161→            try:
   162→                msg = parse_message(raw_data)
   163→            except MessageParseError as e:
   164→                logger.debug("Skipping unrecognized message: %s", e)
   165→                continue
   166→
   167→            if isinstance(msg, StreamEvent):
   168→                event = msg.event
   169→                if not actual_session_id:
   170→                    actual_session_id = msg.session_id
   171→
   172→                event_type = event.get("type", "")
   173→
   174→                if event_type == "content_block_delta":
   175→                    delta = event.get("delta", {})
   176→                    delta_type = delta.get("type", "")
   177→                    if delta_type == "thinking_delta":
   178→                        text = delta.get("thinking", "")
   179→                        if text:
   180→                            current_text += text
   181→                            accumulated_thinking += text
   182→                            yield f"event: thinking\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   183→                    elif delta_type == "text_delta":
   184→                        text = delta.get("text", "")
   185→                        current_text += text
   186→                        accumulated_answer += text
   187→                        event_name = "thinking" if not has_tool_calls else "answer"
   188→                        yield f"event: {event_name}\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   189→
   190→                elif event_type == "content_block_start":
   191→                    block = event.get("content_block", {})
   192→                    block_type = block.get("type")
   193→                    if block_type == "thinking":
   194→                        has_thinking = True
   195→                        # Start a new generation span for this LLM turn
   196→                        if trace:
   197→                            _start_generation()
   198→                    elif block_type == "text":
   199→                        if has_thinking:
   200→                            yield f"event: thinking_done\ndata: {json.dumps({})}\n\n"
   201→                        # If no thinking block preceded, start generation now
   202→                        if not has_thinking and trace:
   203→                            _start_generation()
   204→                    elif block_type == "tool_use":
   205→                        # End the current generation before tool use
   206→                        if trace:
   207→                            _end_generation()
   208→                        # Reset so the next turn correctly detects its own
   209→                        # thinking block (or lack thereof)
   210→                        has_thinking = False
   211→                        if current_text.strip() and not thinking_sent:
   212→                            thinking_sent = True
   213→                        has_tool_calls = True
   214→
   215→            elif isinstance(msg, AssistantMessage):
   216→                if not actual_session_id:
   217→                    actual_session_id = "default"
   218→
   219→                for block in msg.content:
   220→                    if isinstance(block, ToolUseBlock):
   221→                        has_tool_calls = True
   222→                        tool_name = getattr(block, "name", "") or ""
   223→                        tool_names[block.id] = tool_name
   224→                        sql = block.input.get("sql", "")
   225→                        command = block.input.get("command", "")
   226→
   227→                        # Emit tool_call for ALL tool types
   228→                        tool_call_data: dict = {"id": block.id, "name": tool_name}
   229→                        if sql:
   230→                            tool_call_data["sql"] = sql
   231→                        if command:
   232→                            tool_call_data["command"] = command
   233→                        if not sql and not command:
   234→                            tool_call_data["input"] = block.input
   235→                        yield f"event: tool_call\ndata: {json.dumps(tool_call_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   236→
   237→                        # For SQL tools, execute query for structured results
   238→                        if sql:
   239→                            sql_result_ids.add(block.id)
   240→
   241→                            # Create a tool span for the SQL execution
   242→                            tool_span = None
   243→                            if trace:
   244→                                try:
   245→                                    tool_span = trace.start_span(
   246→                                        name=f"tool-{tool_name}",
   247→                                        input={"sql": sql},
   248→                                    )
   249→                                except Exception as e:
   250→                                    logger.debug("Failed to create Langfuse tool span: %s", e)
   251→
   252→                            try:
   253→                                result = db.execute_query(sql)
   254→                                truncated = result["rows"][:100]
   255→                                yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'name': tool_name, 'sql': sql, 'columns': result['columns'], 'rows': truncated, 'rowCount': result['rowCount']}, default=str)}\n\n"
   256→
   257→                                # End tool span with success
   258→                                if tool_span:
   259→                                    try:
   260→                                        tool_span.update(output={
   261→                                            "rowCount": result["rowCount"],
   262→                                            "columns": result["columns"],
   263→                                        })
   264→                                        tool_span.end()
   265→                                    except Exception as e:
   266→                                        logger.debug("Failed to end Langfuse tool span: %s", e)
   267→
   268→                            except Exception as e:
   269→                                yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'name': tool_name, 'sql': sql, 'error': str(e)})}\n\n"
   270→
   271→                                # End tool span with error
   272→                                if tool_span:
   273→                                    try:
   274→                                        tool_span.update(
   275→                                            output={"error": str(e)},
   276→                                            level="ERROR",
   277→                                        )
   278→                                        tool_span.end()
   279→                                    except Exception as ex:
   280→                                        logger.debug("Failed to end Langfuse tool span: %s", ex)
   281→
   282→            elif isinstance(msg, UserMessage):
   283→                # Capture tool results from the SDK for non-SQL tools
   284→                # Note: only SQL tool executions produce Langfuse spans (see above)
   285→                content = msg.content
   286→                if isinstance(content, list):
   287→                    for block in content:
   288→                        if isinstance(block, ToolResultBlock):
   289→                            if block.tool_use_id in sql_result_ids:
   290→                                continue
   291→                            output = _extract_tool_result_text(block.content)
   292→                            name = tool_names.get(block.tool_use_id, "")

   295→                                "name": name,
   296→                                "output": output,
   297→                            }
   298→                            if block.is_error:
   299→                                result_data["error"] = output
   300→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps(result_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   301→
   302→            elif isinstance(msg, ResultMessage):
   303→                actual_session_id = msg.session_id
   304→                if msg.is_error and msg.result:
   305→                    yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': msg.result})}\n\n"
   306→                yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   307→                break
   308→
   309→    except Exception as e:
   310→        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': str(e)})}\n\n"
   311→    finally:
   312→        # End any open generation span
   313→        if trace:
   314→            _end_generation()
   315→
   316→        # Update trace output and flush
   317→        if trace:
   318→            try:
   319→                trace.update_trace(
   320→                    output={"session_id": actual_session_id},
   321→                )
   322→                trace.end()
   323→            except Exception as e:
   324→                logger.debug("Failed to update Langfuse trace: %s", e)
   325→
   326→        if langfuse:
   327→            try:
   328→                langfuse.flush()
   329→            except Exception as e:
   330→                logger.debug("Failed to flush Langfuse: %s", e)
   331→
   332→        try:
   333→            await client.disconnect()
   334→        except Exception:
   335→            pass
   336→

```
     1→# Langfuse Integration Design
     2→
     3→## Goal
     4→
     5→Add Langfuse observability tracing to the agent backend and a "Langfuse Traces" button to the agent mode UI. Langfuse is optional — when not configured, tracing is skipped with zero overhead and the button is shown disabled.
     6→
     7→## Approach
     8→
     9→**@observe decorator + manual spans** — wraps the existing `stream_chat()` function with Langfuse tracing. Each agent chat request produces one trace containing LLM generation spans and tool execution spans. No changes to the SSE streaming protocol.
    10→
    11→## Backend Changes
    12→
    13→### 1. Configuration (`backend/app/config.py`)
    14→
    15→Add environment variables:
    16→
    17→```python
    18→LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY = os.getenv("LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY", "")
    19→LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY = os.getenv("LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY", "")
    20→LANGFUSE_BASE_URL = os.getenv("LANGFUSE_BASE_URL", "https://cloud.langfuse.com")
    21→LANGFUSE_ENABLED = bool(LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY and LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY)
    22→```
    23→
    24→Update `backend/.env.example`:
    25→
    26→```
    27→LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY=
    28→LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY=
    29→LANGFUSE_BASE_URL=https://cloud.langfuse.com
    30→```
    31→
    32→### 2. Tracing Module (`backend/app/tracing.py`)
    33→
    34→New module that:
    35→
    36→- Initializes Langfuse client only when `LANGFUSE_ENABLED` is True
    37→- Verifies connection with `auth_check()` at startup (logs warning on failure)
    38→- Exposes `get_langfuse_client()` returning the client or `None`
    39→- Exposes `get_langfuse_dashboard_url()` returning the base URL or `None`
    40→
    41→### 3. Agent Instrumentation (`backend/app/agent.py`)
    42→
    43→Wrap `stream_chat()` with conditional Langfuse tracing:
    44→
    45→**Trace hierarchy:**
    46→
    47→```
    48→Trace: "agent-chat"
    49→  metadata: { session_id, model, user_message (truncated) }
    50→  ├── Generation: "llm-turn-N"
    51→  │   input: messages context
    52→  │   output: thinking + text content
    53→  │   usage: { input_tokens, output_tokens }
    54→  ├── Span: "tool-execute_sql"
    55→  │   input: { sql }
    56→  │   output: { columns, rows (truncated), rowCount } or { error }
    57→  └── (repeats for multi-turn agent loops)
    58→```
    59→
    60→Key constraints:
    61→- Tracing is conditional — if `get_langfuse_client()` returns `None`, no spans are created
    62→- Zero changes to the SSE event protocol
    63→- Trace captures the session_id as the Langfuse session ID for grouping multi-turn conversations
    64→- Tool results are truncated in traces to avoid sending large datasets to Langfuse
    65→
    66→### 4. API Endpoint (`backend/app/routes/langfuse.py`)
    67→
    68→```
    69→GET /api/langfuse/status
    70→Response: { "enabled": true, "dashboardUrl": "https://cloud.langfuse.com" }
    71→       or { "enabled": false, "dashboardUrl": null }
    72→```
    73→
    74→No secrets exposed. Called once by frontend on app load.
    75→
    76→### 5. App Setup (`backend/app/main.py`)
    77→
    78→Register the new langfuse router.
    79→
    80→### 6. Dependencies (`backend/pyproject.toml`)
    81→
    82→Add `langfuse` package.
    83→
    84→## Frontend Changes
    85→
    86→### 1. Langfuse Status Fetching (`frontend/src/App.tsx`)
    87→
    88→On app load (alongside existing health check), fetch `GET /api/langfuse/status`. Store `{ enabled: boolean, dashboardUrl: string | null }` in state and pass to `AgentPanel`.
    89→
    90→### 2. Langfuse Traces Button (`frontend/src/components/AgentPanel.tsx`)
    91→
    92→Add "Langfuse Traces" button in the agent panel header, between the "Agent Mode" title and "Clear" button:
    93→
    94→```
    95→┌──────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    96→│ Agent Mode    [Langfuse Traces]    [Clear] │
    97→├──────────────────────────────────────────────┤
    98→```
    99→
   100→Button states:
   101→- **Enabled** (Langfuse configured): Subtle link-style button, opens `dashboardUrl` in new tab
   102→- **Disabled** (Langfuse not configured): Grayed out, tooltip "Langfuse not configured"
   103→
   104→### 3. Styles (`frontend/src/components/AgentPanel.css`)
   105→
   106→Add styles for the Langfuse button in both enabled and disabled states. Match existing design language (small text, gray tones, subtle borders).

     1→# Langfuse Integration Implementation Plan
     2→
     3→> **For Claude:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task.
     4→
     5→**Goal:** Add optional Langfuse observability tracing to the agent backend and a "Langfuse Traces" button to the agent mode UI.
     6→
     7→**Architecture:** Backend uses Langfuse Python SDK v3 with `@observe` decorator and manual spans to trace each agent chat request. Frontend fetches Langfuse status from a new API endpoint and renders a button that opens the Langfuse dashboard. Tracing is conditional — zero overhead when Langfuse is not configured.
     8→

    88→### Task 2: Create tracing module

    93→**Step 1: Create the tracing module**
    94→
    95→Create `backend/app/tracing.py`:
    96→
    97→```python
    98→import logging
    99→
   100→from app.config import (
   101→    LANGFUSE_ENABLED,
   102→    LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY,
   103→    LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY,
   104→    LANGFUSE_BASE_URL,
   105→)
   106→
   107→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
   108→
   109→_langfuse_client = None
   110→
   111→
   112→def _init_langfuse():
   113→    """Initialize Langfuse client if configured."""
   114→    global _langfuse_client
   115→    if not LANGFUSE_ENABLED:
   116→        logger.info("Langfuse not configured, tracing disabled")
   117→        return
   118→
   119→    try:
   120→        from langfuse import Langfuse
   121→
   122→        _langfuse_client = Langfuse(
   123→            REDACTED,
   124→            secret_key=LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY,
   125→            base_url=LANGFUSE_BASE_URL,
   126→        )
   127→        if _langfuse_client.auth_check():
   128→            logger.info("Langfuse connected successfully")
   129→        else:
   130→            logger.warning("Langfuse auth check failed, tracing disabled")
   131→            _langfuse_client = None
   132→    except Exception as e:
   133→        logger.warning("Failed to initialize Langfuse: %s", e)
   134→        _langfuse_client = None
   135→
   136→
   137→def get_langfuse_client():
   138→    """Return the Langfuse client or None if not configured."""
   139→    return _langfuse_client
   140→
   141→
   142→def get_langfuse_dashboard_url() -> str | None:
   143→    """Return the Langfuse dashboard base URL or None."""
   144→    if _langfuse_client is None:
   145→        return None
   146→    return LANGFUSE_BASE_URL
   147→
   148→
   149→# Initialize on module import
   150→_init_langfuse()
   151→```

   221→### Task 4: Add Langfuse tracing to agent.py
   222→
   223→**Files:**
   224→- Modify: `backend/app/agent.py:1-197` (add tracing instrumentation)
   225→
   226→This is the core task. The approach wraps the existing `stream_chat()` with Langfuse tracing without changing the SSE protocol.
   227→
   228→**Step 1: Add tracing imports and helper**
   229→
   230→At the top of `backend/app/agent.py`, add the tracing import:
   231→
   232→```python
   233→from app.tracing import get_langfuse_client
   234→```
   235→
   236→**Step 2: Add tracing context to stream_chat**
   237→
   238→Modify `stream_chat()` to create a Langfuse trace at the start and add spans for tool executions. The key changes:
   239→
   240→1. At the start of `stream_chat()`, create a trace if Langfuse is available
   241→2. For each `ToolUseBlock` with SQL, create a tool span
   242→3. Capture LLM text output in a generation span
   243→4. End the trace when the stream completes or errors
   244→
   245→The modified `stream_chat()` function:
   246→
   247→```python
   248→async def stream_chat(message: str, session_id: str | None = None) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
   249→    """Stream agent chat responses as SSE events."""
   250→    duckdb_server = create_duckdb_server()
   251→
   252→    logger.info("Using model: %s", ANTHROPIC_MODEL)
   253→    options = ClaudeAgentOptions(
   254→        model=ANTHROPIC_MODEL,
   255→        system_prompt=build_system_prompt(),
   256→        mcp_servers={"duckdb": duckdb_server},
   257→        allowed_tools=["mcp__duckdb__execute_sql"],
   258→        permission_mode="bypassPermissions",
   259→        max_turns=20,
   260→        include_partial_messages=True,
   261→    )
   262→
   263→    client = ClaudeSDKClient(options=options)
   264→    actual_session_id = session_id
   265→
   266→    # Langfuse tracing (conditional)
   267→    langfuse = get_langfuse_client()
   268→    trace = None
   269→    current_generation = None
   270→    llm_turn = 0
   271→    accumulated_thinking = ""
   272→    accumulated_answer = ""
   273→
   274→    if langfuse:
   275→        trace = langfuse.trace(
   276→            name="agent-chat",
   277→            session_id=session_id or "default",
   278→            input={"message": message[:500]},
   279→            metadata={"model": ANTHROPIC_MODEL},
   280→        )
   281→
   282→    try:
   283→        await client.connect()
   284→        await client.query(message, session_id=session_id or "default")
   285→
   286→        current_text = ""
   287→        has_tool_calls = False
   288→        thinking_sent = False
   289→        has_thinking = False
   290→        sql_result_ids: set[str] = set()
   291→        tool_names: dict[str, str] = {}
   292→
   293→        async for raw_data in client._query.receive_messages():
   294→            try:
   295→                msg = parse_message(raw_data)
   296→            except MessageParseError as e:
   297→                logger.debug("Skipping unrecognized message: %s", e)
   298→                continue
   299→
   300→            if isinstance(msg, StreamEvent):
   301→                event = msg.event
   302→                if not actual_session_id:
   303→                    actual_session_id = msg.session_id
   304→
   305→                event_type = event.get("type", "")
   306→
   307→                if event_type == "content_block_delta":
   308→                    delta = event.get("delta", {})
   309→                    delta_type = delta.get("type", "")
   310→                    if delta_type == "thinking_delta":
   311→                        text = delta.get("thinking", "")
   312→                        if text:
   313→                            current_text += text
   314→                            accumulated_thinking += text
   315→                            yield f"event: thinking\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   316→                    elif delta_type == "text_delta":
   317→                        text = delta.get("text", "")
   318→                        current_text += text
   319→                        accumulated_answer += text
   320→                        event_name = "thinking" if not has_tool_calls else "answer"
   321→                        yield f"event: {event_name}\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   322→
   323→                elif event_type == "content_block_start":
   324→                    block = event.get("content_block", {})
   325→                    block_type = block.get("type")
   326→                    if block_type == "thinking":
   327→                        has_thinking = True
   328→                        # Start a new generation span for this LLM turn
   329→                        if trace:
   330→                            llm_turn += 1
   331→                            current_generation = trace.generation(
   332→                                name=f"llm-turn-{llm_turn}",
   333→                                model=ANTHROPIC_MODEL,
   334→                                input={"message": message[:500]} if llm_turn == 1 else {"continued": True},
   335→                            )
   336→                    elif block_type == "text":
   337→                        if has_thinking:
   338→                            yield f"event: thinking_done\ndata: {json.dumps({})}\n\n"
   339→                        elif trace and not current_generation:
   340→                            # Text block without thinking — start generation
   341→                            llm_turn += 1
   342→                            current_generation = trace.generation(
   343→                                name=f"llm-turn-{llm_turn}",
   344→                                model=ANTHROPIC_MODEL,
   345→                                input={"message": message[:500]} if llm_turn == 1 else {"continued": True},
   346→                            )
   347→                    elif block_type == "tool_use":
   348→                        if current_text.strip() and not thinking_sent:
   349→                            thinking_sent = True
   350→                        has_tool_calls = True
   351→                        # End current generation before tool use
   352→                        if current_generation:
   353→                            current_generation.end(
   354→                                output={"thinking": accumulated_thinking[:1000], "text": accumulated_answer[:1000]},
   355→                            )
   356→                            current_generation = None
   357→                            accumulated_thinking = ""
   358→                            accumulated_answer = ""
   359→
   360→            elif isinstance(msg, AssistantMessage):
   361→                if not actual_session_id:
   362→                    actual_session_id = "default"
   363→
   364→                for block in msg.content:
   365→                    if isinstance(block, ToolUseBlock):
   366→                        has_tool_calls = True
   367→                        tool_name = getattr(block, "name", "") or ""
   368→                        tool_names[block.id] = tool_name
   369→                        sql = block.input.get("sql", "")
   370→                        command = block.input.get("command", "")
   371→
   372→                        # Emit tool_call for ALL tool types
   373→                        tool_call_data: dict = {"id": block.id, "name": tool_name}
   374→                        if sql:
   375→                            tool_call_data["sql"] = sql
   376→                        if command:
   377→                            tool_call_data["command"] = command
   378→                        if not sql and not command:
   379→                            tool_call_data["input"] = block.input
   380→                        yield f"event: tool_call\ndata: {json.dumps(tool_call_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   381→
   382→                        # For SQL tools, execute query for structured results
   383→                        if sql:
   384→                            sql_result_ids.add(block.id)
   385→
   386→                            # Create tool span in trace
   387→                            tool_span = trace.span(
   388→                                name=f"tool-{tool_name}",
   389→                                input={"sql": sql},
   390→                            ) if trace else None
   391→
   392→                            try:
   393→                                result = db.execute_query(sql)
   394→                                truncated = result["rows"][:100]
   395→                                yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'name': tool_name, 'sql': sql, 'columns': result['columns'], 'rows': truncated, 'rowCount': result['rowCount']}, default=str)}\n\n"
   396→                                if tool_span:
   397→                                    tool_span.end(output={"rowCount": result["rowCount"], "columns": result["columns"]})
   398→                            except Exception as e:
   399→                                yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'name': tool_name, 'sql': sql, 'error': str(e)})}\n\n"
   400→                                if tool_span:
   401→                                    tool_span.end(output={"error": str(e)}, level="ERROR")
   402→
   403→            elif isinstance(msg, UserMessage):
   404→                # Capture tool results from the SDK for non-SQL tools
   405→                content = msg.content
   406→                if isinstance(content, list):
   407→                    for block in content:
   408→                        if isinstance(block, ToolResultBlock):
   409→                            if block.tool_use_id in sql_result_ids:
   410→                                continue
   411→                            output = _extract_tool_result_text(block.content)
   412→                            name = tool_names.get(block.tool_use_id, "")
   413→                            result_data: dict = {
   414→                                "id": block.tool_use_id,
   415→                                "name": name,
   416→                                "output": output,
   417→                            }
   418→                            if block.is_error:
   419→                                result_data["error"] = output
   420→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps(result_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   421→
   422→            elif isinstance(msg, ResultMessage):
   423→                actual_session_id = msg.session_id
   424→                if msg.is_error and msg.result:
   425→                    yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': msg.result})}\n\n"
   426→                yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   427→                break
   428→
   429→    except Exception as e:
   430→        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': str(e)})}\n\n"
   431→    finally:
   432→        # End any open generation span
   433→        if current_generation:
   434→            current_generation.end(
   435→                output={"thinking": accumulated_thinking[:1000], "text": accumulated_answer[:1000]},
   436→            )
   437→        # Finalize trace
   438→        if trace:
   439→            trace.update(output={"session_id": actual_session_id})
   440→        if langfuse:
   441→            langfuse.flush()
   442→        try:
   443→            await client.disconnect()
   444→        except Exception:
   445→            pass
   446→```

## Langfuse API Search Report

I've thoroughly searched the installed langfuse package (v3.14.4) in your backend poetry venv. Here are the findings:

### 1. `get_client` Function ✓ FOUND

**Import Path:** `from langfuse import get_client`

**File Location:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/langfuse/_client/get_client.py`

**Signature:**
```python
def get_client(*, public_key: Optional[str] = None) -> Langfuse
```

**Key Features:**
- Returns a Langfuse client instance using singleton pattern per public_key
- Supports multi-project setups with public_key parameter
- Returns disabled client for safety if multiple projects exist without explicit key
- Context-aware: checks execution context for public key via ContextVar

**Usage Pattern:**
```python
# Single project
client = get_client()

# Multi-project
client_a = get_client(public_key="project_a_key")
client_b = get_client(public_key="project_b_key")
```

---

### 2. `start_as_current_observation` ✓ FOUND

**Location:** `Langfuse.start_as_current_observation()` method

**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/langfuse/_client/client.py` (line 1105)

**Full Signature:**
```python
def start_as_current_observation(
    self,
    *,
    trace_context: Optional[TraceContext] = None,
    name: str,
    as_type: ObservationTypeLiteralNoEvent = "span",
    input: Optional[Any] = None,
    output: Optional[Any] = None,
    metadata: Optional[Any] = None,
    version: Optional[str] = None,
    level: Optional[Literal['DEBUG', 'DEFAULT', 'WARNING', 'ERROR']] = None,
    status_message: Optional[str] = None,
    completion_start_time: Optional[datetime.datetime] = None,
    model: Optional[str] = None,
    model_parameters: Optional[Dict[str, Union[str, NoneType, int, bool, List[str]]]] = None,
    usage_details: Optional[Dict[str, int]] = None,
    cost_details: Optional[Dict[str, float]] = None,
    prompt: Union[langfuse.model.TextPromptClient, langfuse.model.ChatPromptClient, NoneType] = None,
    end_on_exit: Optional[bool] = None,
) -> _AgnosticContextManager[LangfuseObservation]
```

**Supported `as_type` Values:**
- `'span'` (default) → returns `_AgnosticContextManager[LangfuseSpan]`
- `'generation'` → returns `_AgnosticContextManager[LangfuseGeneration]`
- `'embedding'` → returns `_AgnosticContextManager[LangfuseEmbedding]`
- `'agent'` → returns `_AgnosticContextManager[LangfuseAgent]`
- `'tool'` → returns `_AgnosticContextManager[LangfuseTool]`
- `'chain'` → returns `_AgnosticContextManager[LangfuseChain]`
- `'retriever'` → returns `_AgnosticContextManager[LangfuseRetriever]`
- `'evaluator'` → returns `_AgnosticContextManager[LangfuseEvaluator]`
- `'guardrail'` → returns `_AgnosticContextManager[LangfuseGuardrail]`

**Key Features:**
- Context manager that automatically sets as current span
- Supports optional trace_context for connecting to existing traces
- Auto-ends span on exit unless `end_on_exit=False`
- Type-aware: different parameters available based on `as_type`

**Usage Example:**
```python
with langfuse.start_as_current_observation(name="process-query", as_type="span") as span:
    result = process_data()
    span.update(output=result)

with langfuse.start_as_current_observation(
    name="answer-generation",
    as_type="generation",
    model="gpt-4"
) as generation:
    response = llm.generate(...)
    generation.update(output=response)
```

---

### 3. `propagate_attributes` ✓ FOUND

**Import Path:** `from langfuse import propagate_attributes`

**File Location:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/langfuse/_client/propagation.py`

**Signature:**
```python
def propagate_attributes(
    *,
    user_id: Optional[str] = None,
    session_id: Optional[str] = None,
    metadata: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None,
    version: Optional[str] = None,
    tags: Optional[List[str]] = None,
    trace_name: Optional[str] = None,
    as_baggage: bool = False,
) -> _AgnosticContextManager[Any]
```

**Key Features:**
- Context manager for propagating trace-level attributes to all child spans
- Uses OpenTelemetry under the hood
- Supports baggage propagation for cross-service tracing (with security warnings)
- **IMPORTANT**: Must be called early in trace - only affects spans created after entering context
- All string values must be ≤200 characters
- Metadata keys and values must be US-ASCII strings

**Propagated Attributes:**
- `user_id`: User identifier (≤200 chars)
- `session_id`: Session identifier for grouping traces (≤200 chars)
- `metadata`: Dict[str, str] with keys/values ≤200 chars each
- `version`: Version identifier
- `tags`: List[str] for categorization
- `trace_name`: Name to assign to trace (≤200 chars)
- `as_baggage`: Enable OpenTelemetry baggage propagation

**Usage Example:**
```python
from langfuse import propagate_attributes

with langfuse.start_as_current_span(name="user_workflow") as span:
    with propagate_attributes(
        user_id="user_123",
        session_id="session_abc",
        metadata={"experiment": "variant_a", "environment": "production"}
    ):
        # All spans created here inherit these attributes
        with langfuse.start_span(name="llm_call") as llm_span:
            ...
```

---

### 4. `update_current_trace` ✓ FOUND

**Location:** `Langfuse.update_current_trace()` method

**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/langfuse/_client/client.py` (line 1643)

**Signature:**
```python
def update_current_trace(
    self,
    *,
    name: Optional[str] = None,
    user_id: Optional[str] = None,
    session_id: Optional[str] = None,
    version: Optional[str] = None,
    input: Optional[Any] = None,
    output: Optional[Any] = None,
    metadata: Optional[Any] = None,
    tags: Optional[List[str]] = None,
    public: Optional[bool] = None,
) -> None
```

**Key Features:**
- Updates the current trace with additional information
- Can set trace name, user context, session, and metadata
- Recommended alternative: `propagate_attributes()` for setting trace-level attributes
- Returns None

**Parameters:**
- `name`: Updated trace name
- `user_id`: User who initiated the trace
- `session_id`: Session identifier for grouping traces
- `version`: Version identifier
- `input`: Input data for the trace
- `output`: Output data from the trace
- `metadata`: Additional metadata dict
- `tags`: List of tags for categorization
- `public`: Whether trace is publicly accessible

**Usage Example:**
```python
with langfuse.start_as_current_span(name="workflow") as span:
    # Do work...
    langfuse.update_current_trace(
        name="Updated Trace Name",
        user_id="user_123",
        output={"result": "success"}
    )
```

```
def configure_claude_agent_sdk(
    name: Optional[str] = None,
    project_name: Optional[str] = None,
    metadata: Optional[dict] = None,
    tags: Optional[list[str]] = None,
) -> bool:
    """Enable LangSmith tracing for the Claude Agent SDK by patching entry points.

    This function instruments the Claude Agent SDK to automatically trace:
    - Chain runs for each conversation stream (via `ClaudeSDKClient`)
    - Model runs for each assistant turn
    - All tool calls including built-in tools, external MCP tools, and SDK MCP tools

    Tool tracing is implemented via `PreToolUse` and `PostToolUse` hooks

    Args:
        name: Name of the root trace.
        project_name: LangSmith project to trace to.
        metadata: Metadata to associate with all traces.
        tags: Tags to associate with all traces.

    Returns:
        `True` if configuration was successful, `False` otherwise.

    Example:
        >>> from langsmith.integrations.claude_agent_sdk import (
        ...     configure_claude_agent_sdk,
        ... )
        >>> configure_claude_agent_sdk(
        ...     project_name="my-project", tags=["production"]
        ... )  # doctest: +SKIP
        >>> # Now use claude_agent_sdk as normal - tracing is automatic
    """  # noqa: E501
    try:
        import claude_agent_sdk  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
    except ImportError:
        logger.warning("Claude Agent SDK not installed.")
        return False

    if not hasattr(claude_agent_sdk, "ClaudeSDKClient"):
        logger.warning("Claude Agent SDK missing ClaudeSDKClient.")
        return False

    set_tracing_config(
        name=name,
        project_name=project_name,
        metadata=metadata,
        tags=tags,
    )

    original = getattr(claude_agent_sdk, "ClaudeSDKClient", None)
    if not original:
        return False

    wrapped = instrument_claude_client(original)
    setattr(claude_agent_sdk, "ClaudeSDKClient", wrapped)

    for module in list(sys.modules.values()):
        try:
            if module and getattr(module, "ClaudeSDKClient", None) is original:
                setattr(module, "ClaudeSDKClient", wrapped)
        except Exception:
            continue

    return True
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/tracing.py",
  "content": "import logging\nimport os\n\nfrom app.config import LANGFUSE_ENABLED, LANGFUSE_BASE_URL\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n_langfuse_client = None\n\n\ndef _init_langfuse():\n    \"\"\"Initialize Langfuse with OTel auto-instrumentation for Claude Agent SDK.\"\"\"\n    global _langfuse_client\n    if not LANGFUSE_ENABLED:\n        logger.info(\"Langfuse not configured, tracing disabled\")\n        return\n\n    # Set OTel env vars so langsmith routes traces through Langfuse\n    os.environ.setdefault(\"LANGSMITH_OTEL_ENABLED\", \"true\")\n    os.environ.setdefault(\"LANGSMITH_OTEL_ONLY\", \"true\")\n    os.environ.setdefault(\"LANGSMITH_TRACING\", \"true\")\n\n    try:\n        from langfuse import get_client\n\n        _langfuse_client = get_client()\n\n        if _langfuse_client.auth_check():\n            logger.info(\"Langfuse connected successfully\")\n        else:\n            logger.warning(\"Langfuse auth check failed, tracing disabled\")\n            _langfuse_client = None\n            return\n\n        # Enable auto-instrumentation for Claude Agent SDK\n        from langsmith.integrations.claude_agent_sdk import configure_claude_agent_sdk\n\n        if configure_claude_agent_sdk():\n            logger.info(\"Claude Agent SDK auto-instrumentation enabled\")\n        else:\n            logger.warning(\n                \"Failed to configure Claude Agent SDK auto-instrumentation\"\n            )\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.warning(\"Failed to initialize Langfuse: %s\", e)\n        _langfuse_client = None\n\n\ndef get_langfuse_client():\n    \"\"\"Return the Langfuse client or None if not configured.\"\"\"\n    return _langfuse_client\n\n\ndef get_langfuse_dashboard_url() -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"Return the Langfuse project traces URL or None.\"\"\"\n    if _langfuse_client is None:\n        return None\n    try:\n        project_id = _langfuse_client._get_project_id()\n        if project_id:\n            return f\"{LANGFUSE_BASE_URL}/project/{project_id}/traces\"\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.warning(\"Failed to get Langfuse project ID: %s\", e)\n    return LANGFUSE_BASE_URL\n\n\n# Initialize on module import\n_init_langfuse()\n"
}

### Task 4: Simplify agent tracing

The plan says to:
- **Remove**: `_truncate()`, all tracing state, `_start_generation()`/`_end_generation()`, manual generation/tool span tracking, trace finalization
- **Add**: `start_as_current_observation()` context, `propagate_attributes()`, `update_current_trace()`, `flush()`
- **Keep**: `_extract_tool_result_text()`, `build_system_prompt()`, all SSE streaming logic, SQL execution

{
  "file_path": "/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/agent.py",
  "content": "import json\nimport logging\nfrom typing import AsyncIterator\n\nfrom claude_agent_sdk import (\n    ClaudeSDKClient,\n    ClaudeAgentOptions,\n    AssistantMessage,\n    UserMessage,\n    ResultMessage,\n    ToolUseBlock,\n    ToolResultBlock,\n)\nfrom claude_agent_sdk._errors import MessageParseError\nfrom claude_agent_sdk._internal.message_parser import parse_message\nfrom claude_agent_sdk.types import StreamEvent\nfrom app.tools import create_duckdb_server\nfrom app.database import db\nfrom app.config import ANTHROPIC_MODEL\nfrom app.tracing import get_langfuse_client\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\ndef build_system_prompt() -> str:\n    tables = db.list_tables()\n    prompt = \"\"\"You are a helpful data analyst assistant working with a DuckDB database.\nYou can execute SQL queries using the execute_sql tool to answer questions about the user's data.\n\nGuidelines:\n- Write clear, efficient DuckDB SQL queries\n- When exploring data, start with small queries (use LIMIT)\n- Explain your findings in plain language after getting results\n- If a query fails, try to fix it and retry\n- Use double quotes for table and column names that might conflict with reserved words\n\"\"\"\n    if not tables:\n        prompt += \"\\nNo tables are currently loaded. Ask the user to upload a CSV file first.\"\n    else:\n        prompt += \"\\nCurrently loaded tables:\\n\"\n        for table in tables:\n            prompt += f'\\nTable: \"{table[\"name\"]}\" ({table[\"rowCount\"]} rows)\\nColumns:\\n'\n            for col in table[\"columns\"]:\n                prompt += f'  - \"{col[\"name\"]}\" ({col[\"type\"]})\\n'\n    return prompt\n\n\ndef _extract_tool_result_text(content: object) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Extract text from ToolResultBlock.content.\"\"\"\n    if content is None:\n        return \"\"\n    if isinstance(content, str):\n        return content\n    if isinstance(content, list):\n        parts = []\n        for item in content:\n            if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get(\"type\") == \"text\":\n                parts.append(item.get(\"text\", \"\"))\n        return \"\\n\".join(parts)\n    return str(content)\n\n\nasync def stream_chat(message: str, session_id: str | None = None) -> AsyncIterator[str]:\n    \"\"\"Stream agent chat responses as SSE events.\"\"\"\n    duckdb_server = create_duckdb_server()\n\n    logger.info(\"Using model: %s\", ANTHROPIC_MODEL)\n    options = ClaudeAgentOptions(\n        model=ANTHROPIC_MODEL,\n        system_prompt=build_system_prompt(),\n        mcp_servers={\"duckdb\": duckdb_server},\n        allowed_tools=[\"mcp__duckdb__execute_sql\"],\n        permission_mode=\"bypassPermissions\",\n        max_turns=20,\n        include_partial_messages=True,\n    )\n\n    client = ClaudeSDKClient(options=options)\n    actual_session_id = session_id\n\n    # --- Langfuse OTel tracing setup (conditional) ---\n    langfuse = get_langfuse_client()\n    observation_ctx = None\n    if langfuse:\n        try:\n            from langfuse import propagate_attributes\n\n            observation_ctx = langfuse.start_as_current_observation(\n                name=\"agent-chat\",\n                input={\"message\": message[:500]},\n                metadata={\"model\": ANTHROPIC_MODEL},\n            )\n            observation_ctx.__enter__()\n\n            # Propagate session_id so auto-instrumented child spans inherit it\n            propagate_attributes(\n                session_id=session_id or \"default\",\n            ).__enter__()\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.debug(\"Failed to set up Langfuse tracing context: %s\", e)\n            observation_ctx = None\n\n    try:\n        await client.connect()\n        await client.query(message, session_id=session_id or \"default\")\n\n        current_text = \"\"\n        has_tool_calls = False\n        thinking_sent = False\n        has_thinking = False\n        sql_result_ids: set[str] = set()\n        tool_names: dict[str, str] = {}\n\n        async for raw_data in client._query.receive_messages():\n            try:\n                msg = parse_message(raw_data)\n            except MessageParseError as e:\n                logger.debug(\"Skipping unrecognized message: %s\", e)\n                continue\n\n            if isinstance(msg, StreamEvent):\n                event = msg.event\n                if not actual_session_id:\n                    actual_session_id = msg.session_id\n\n                event_type = event.get(\"type\", \"\")\n\n                if event_type == \"content_block_delta\":\n                    delta = event.get(\"delta\", {})\n                    delta_type = delta.get(\"type\", \"\")\n                    if delta_type == \"thinking_delta\":\n                        text = delta.get(\"thinking\", \"\")\n                        if text:\n                            current_text += text\n                            yield f\"event: thinking\\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\\n\\n\"\n                    elif delta_type == \"text_delta\":\n                        text = delta.get(\"text\", \"\")\n                        current_text += text\n                        event_name = \"thinking\" if not has_tool_calls else \"answer\"\n                        yield f\"event: {event_name}\\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\\n\\n\"\n\n                elif event_type == \"content_block_start\":\n                    block = event.get(\"content_block\", {})\n                    block_type = block.get(\"type\")\n                    if block_type == \"thinking\":\n                        has_thinking = True\n                    elif block_type == \"text\":\n                        if has_thinking:\n                            yield f\"event: thinking_done\\ndata: {json.dumps({})}\\n\\n\"\n                    elif block_type == \"tool_use\":\n                        has_thinking = False\n                        if current_text.strip() and not thinking_sent:\n                            thinking_sent = True\n                        has_tool_calls = True\n\n            elif isinstance(msg, AssistantMessage):\n                if not actual_session_id:\n                    actual_session_id = \"default\"\n\n                for block in msg.content:\n                    if isinstance(block, ToolUseBlock):\n                        has_tool_calls = True\n                        tool_name = getattr(block, \"name\", \"\") or \"\"\n                        tool_names[block.id] = tool_name\n                        sql = block.input.get(\"sql\", \"\")\n                        command = block.input.get(\"command\", \"\")\n\n                        # Emit tool_call for ALL tool types\n                        tool_call_data: dict = {\"id\": block.id, \"name\": tool_name}\n                        if sql:\n                            tool_call_data[\"sql\"] = sql\n                        if command:\n                            tool_call_data[\"command\"] = command\n                        if not sql and not command:\n                            tool_call_data[\"input\"] = block.input\n                        yield f\"event: tool_call\\ndata: {json.dumps(tool_call_data, default=str)}\\n\\n\"\n\n                        # For SQL tools, execute query for structured results\n                        if sql:\n                            sql_result_ids.add(block.id)\n                            try:\n                                result = db.execute_query(sql)\n                                truncated = result[\"rows\"][:100]\n                                yield f\"event: tool_result\\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'name': tool_name, 'sql': sql, 'columns': result['columns'], 'rows': truncated, 'rowCount': result['rowCount']}, default=str)}\\n\\n\"\n                            except Exception as e:\n                                yield f\"event: tool_result\\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'name': tool_name, 'sql': sql, 'error': str(e)})}\\n\\n\"\n\n            elif isinstance(msg, UserMessage):\n                # Capture tool results from the SDK for non-SQL tools\n                content = msg.content\n                if isinstance(content, list):\n                    for block in content:\n                        if isinstance(block, ToolResultBlock):\n                            if block.tool_use_id in sql_result_ids:\n                                continue\n                            output = _extract_tool_result_text(block.content)\n                            name = tool_names.get(block.tool_use_id, \"\")\n                            result_data: dict = {\n                                \"id\": block.tool_use_id,\n                                \"name\": name,\n                                \"output\": output,\n                            }\n                            if block.is_error:\n                                result_data[\"error\"] = output\n                            yield f\"event: tool_result\\ndata: {json.dumps(result_data, default=str)}\\n\\n\"\n\n            elif isinstance(msg, ResultMessage):\n                actual_session_id = msg.session_id\n                if msg.is_error and msg.result:\n                    yield f\"event: error\\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': msg.result})}\\n\\n\"\n                yield f\"event: done\\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\\n\\n\"\n                break\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        yield f\"event: error\\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': str(e)})}\\n\\n\"\n    finally:\n        # Update trace output and end observation context\n        if langfuse and observation_ctx:\n            try:\n                langfuse.update_current_trace(\n                    output={\"session_id\": actual_session_id},\n                )\n                observation_ctx.__exit__(None, None, None)\n            except Exception as e:\n                logger.debug(\"Failed to finalize Langfuse trace: %s\", e)\n            try:\n                langfuse.flush()\n            except Exception as e:\n                logger.debug(\"Failed to flush Langfuse: %s\", e)\n\n        try:\n            await client.disconnect()\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n"
}

  "old_string": "### 2. Tracing Module (`backend/app/tracing.py`)\n\nNew module that:\n\n- Initializes Langfuse client only when `LANGFUSE_ENABLED` is True\n- Verifies connection with `auth_check()` at startup (logs warning on failure)\n- Exposes `get_langfuse_client()` returning the client or `None`\n- Exposes `get_langfuse_dashboard_url()` returning the base URL or `None`\n\n### 3. Agent Instrumentation (`backend/app/agent.py`)\n\nWrap `stream_chat()` with conditional Langfuse tracing:\n\n**Trace hierarchy:**\n\n```\nTrace: \"agent-chat\"\n  metadata: { session_id, model, user_message (truncated) }\n  ├── Generation: \"llm-turn-N\"\n  │   input: messages context\n  │   output: thinking + text content\n  │   usage: { input_tokens, output_tokens }\n  ├── Span: \"tool-execute_sql\"\n  │   input: { sql }\n  │   output: { columns, rows (truncated), rowCount } or { error }\n  └── (repeats for multi-turn agent loops)\n```\n\nKey constraints:\n- Tracing is conditional — if `get_langfuse_client()` returns `None`, no spans are created\n- Zero changes to the SSE event protocol\n- Trace captures the session_id as the Langfuse session ID for grouping multi-turn conversations\n- Tool results are truncated in traces to avoid sending large datasets to Langfuse",
  "new_string": "### 2. Tracing Module (`backend/app/tracing.py`)\n\nModule that:\n\n- Uses `langfuse.get_client()` singleton (reads from env vars) when `LANGFUSE_ENABLED` is True\n- Verifies connection with `auth_check()` at startup (logs warning on failure)\n- Calls `configure_claude_agent_sdk()` from `langsmith.integrations.claude_agent_sdk` to enable auto-instrumentation\n- Sets OTel env vars (`LANGSMITH_OTEL_ENABLED`, `LANGSMITH_OTEL_ONLY`, `LANGSMITH_TRACING`) programmatically\n- Exposes `get_langfuse_client()` returning the client or `None`\n- Exposes `get_langfuse_dashboard_url()` returning the project traces URL or `None`\n\n### 3. Agent Instrumentation (`backend/app/agent.py`)\n\nMinimal tracing context in `stream_chat()` — auto-instrumentation handles the rest:\n\n- `start_as_current_observation()` creates the parent \"agent-chat\" observation\n- `propagate_attributes()` sets session_id for trace grouping\n- `update_current_trace()` records trace output\n- `flush()` ensures traces are sent before response ends\n\nLLM generations, tool calls, and token usage are automatically captured by the langsmith integration. No manual generation or tool spans needed.\n\nKey constraints:\n- Tracing is conditional — if `get_langfuse_client()` returns `None`, no context is created\n- Zero changes to the SSE event protocol\n- SQL execution for frontend display remains unchanged (auto-instrumentation captures MCP execution separately)"
}
```

Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive understanding. Let me create a summary document by analyzing what I found:

## Investigation Summary

Based on my thorough investigation of both the LangFuse and LangSmith packages, I've identified the complete flow of how OTel traces are supposed to get routed from LangSmith to Langfuse. Here are my findings:

### 1. **Langfuse's OTel Setup (`get_client()` function)**

**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/langfuse/_client/get_client.py`

- `get_client()` does NOT register a global TracerProvider
- It only returns Langfuse client instances (singletons per public_key)
- The actual TracerProvider setup happens in `LangfuseResourceManager`

**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/langfuse/_client/resource_manager.py` (lines 179-202)

Critical discovery:
```python
def _init_tracer_provider(...) -> TracerProvider:
    default_provider = cast(TracerProvider, otel_trace_api.get_tracer_provider())
    
    if isinstance(default_provider, otel_trace_api.ProxyTracerProvider):
        provider = TracerProvider(
            resource=resource,
            sampler=TraceIdRatioBased(sample_rate) if sample_rate < 1 else None,
        )
        otel_trace_api.set_tracer_provider(provider)  # ← REGISTERS GLOBAL TRACER PROVIDER
    else:
        provider = default_provider  # ← USES EXISTING PROVIDER
    
    return provider
```

**Key Point:** If there's already a global TracerProvider (not ProxyTracerProvider), Langfuse REUSES it instead of creating its own.

### 2. **LangSmith's OTel Exporter Setup**

**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/langsmith/client.py` (lines 1031-1072)

When `OTEL_ENABLED=true` is set, LangSmith:

```python
existing_provider = otel_trace.get_tracer_provider()
tracer = existing_provider.get_tracer(__name__)

if otel_tracer_provider is None:
    # Use existing global provider if available
    if not (isinstance(existing_provider, otel_trace.ProxyTracerProvider) ...):
        otel_tracer_provider = cast(TracerProvider, existing_provider)
    else:
        otel_tracer_provider = get_otlp_tracer_provider()  # ← CREATES NEW PROVIDER
        otel_trace.set_tracer_provider(otel_tracer_provider)

self.otel_exporter = OTELExporter(tracer_provider=otel_tracer_provider)
```

### 3. **LangSmith's OTel Exporter (`OTELExporter` class)**

**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/langsmith/_internal/otel/_otel_exporter.py`

Key findings:
- **Line 167-169:** Uses the provided `tracer_provider` (or the global one if not provided)
- **Lines 248-249:** Creates deterministic OTel trace and span IDs from UUIDs
- **Line 287-302:** Creates spans with deterministic IDs in the OTel tracer

```python
def __init__(self, tracer_provider=None, span_ttl_seconds=None):
    ...
    self._tracer = trace.get_tracer(
        "langsmith", tracer_provider=tracer_provider
    )
```

The exporter calls `export_batch()` which creates OTel spans directly (not exporting to HTTP endpoint).

### 4. **LangSmith's Tracer Provider Configuration**

**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/langsmith/_internal/otel/_otel_client.py`

The `get_otlp_tracer_provider()` function (lines 45-106) sets up:
```python
OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT = {api_endpoint}/otel  # ← POINTS TO LANGSMITH'S OTEL
os.environ["OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT"] = f"{ls_endpoint}/otel"
os.environ["OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_HEADERS"] = f"x-api-key={api_key}"

otlp_exporter = OTLPSpanExporter()  # ← Uses OTEL SDK's HTTP exporter
tracer_provider.add_span_processor(BatchSpanProcessor(otlp_exporter))
```

### 5. **Langfuse's OTel Endpoint**

**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/langfuse/api/resources/opentelemetry/client.py` (lines 26-164)

Langfuse provides an OTel endpoint at: **`api/public/otel/v1/traces`**

This endpoint:
- Accepts OTLP/HTTP format (protobuf or JSON)
- Implements the OpenTelemetry spec
- Receives ResourceSpans with trace data

### 6. **The Root Cause: TracerProvider Conflict**

**The Problem:**
When both Langfuse and LangSmith initialize:

1. **Langfuse initializes first** (when you call `Langfuse()` or `get_client()`)
   - Checks if there's a global TracerProvider
   - If only ProxyTracerProvider exists, it creates a NEW `TracerProvider` and sets it globally
   - This NEW provider has **no span processors** configured

2. **LangSmith initializes next** (when Client is created with OTEL_ENABLED=true)
   - Checks if there's a global TracerProvider
   - If one exists (Langfuse's), it REUSES it
   - BUT LangSmith expects to configure the tracer with OTLP exporter
   - **The issue:** LangSmith's `OTELExporter` creates spans directly via tracer (doesn't use HTTP export)
   - The reused Langfuse provider has NO span processors to actually export the spans

**Missing Configuration:**
The Langfuse TracerProvider created at lines 444-449 of resource_manager.py:
```python
provider = TracerProvider(
    resource=resource,
    sampler=TraceIdRatioBased(sample_rate) if sample_rate < 1 else None,
)
otel_trace_api.set_tracer_provider(provider)
```

**Has NO span processors.** It's just a bare TracerProvider that records spans to memory but doesn't export them anywhere.

### 7. **How to Fix This**

The root cause is that:
1. LangSmith's `OTELExporter` creates spans directly in the OTel SDK
2. But Langfuse's TracerProvider (which gets reused) has no way to export those spans
3. The spans are created but never sent to Langfuse's HTTP endpoint

**Solution approaches:**
1. Make Langfuse register a span processor that exports to Langfuse's `/api/public/otel/v1/traces` endpoint when OTel is enabled
2. Or ensure LangSmith knows about Langfuse's endpoint and creates its own exporter
3. Or have explicit integration where both libraries share the same OTLP exporter

def is_env_var_truish(value: Optional[str]) -> bool:
    """Check if the given environment variable is truish."""
    if value is None:
        return False
    return is_truish(get_env_var(value))

```
@functools.lru_cache(maxsize=100)
def get_env_var(
    name: str,
    default: Optional[str] = None,
    *,
    namespaces: tuple = ("LANGSMITH", "LANGCHAIN"),
) -> Optional[str]:
    """Retrieve an environment variable from a list of namespaces.

    Args:
        name: The name of the environment variable.
        default: The default value to return if the environment variable is not found.
        namespaces: A tuple of namespaces to search for the environment variable.

            Defaults to `('LANGSMITH', 'LANGCHAINs')`.

    Returns:
        The value of the environment variable if found, otherwise the default value.
    """
    names = [f"{namespace}_{name}" for namespace in namespaces]
    for name in names:
        value = os.environ.get(name)
        if value is not None and value.strip() != "":
            return value
    return default
```

def instrument_claude_client(original_class: Any) -> Any:
    """Wrap `ClaudeSDKClient` to trace both `query()` and `receive_response()`."""
    if getattr(original_class, "_langsmith_instrumented", False):
        return original_class  # Already wrapped, avoid double-tracing

    class TracedClaudeSDKClient(original_class):
        _langsmith_instrumented = True

        def __init__(self, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any):
            # Inject LangSmith tracing hooks into options before initialization
            options = kwargs.get("options") or (args[0] if args else None)
            if options:
                _inject_tracing_hooks(options)

            super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
            self._prompt: Optional[str] = None
            self._start_time: Optional[float] = None
            self._streamed_input: Optional[list[dict[str, Any]]] = None

        async def query(self, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
            """Capture prompt and start time, wrapping generators if needed."""
            self._start_time = time.time()
            self._streamed_input = None
            prompt = args[0] if args else kwargs.get("prompt")

            if prompt is None:
                pass
            elif isinstance(prompt, str):
                self._prompt = prompt
            elif isinstance(prompt, AsyncIterable):
                collector: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
                self._streamed_input = collector
                self._prompt = None

                async def _gen_wrapper() -> AsyncGenerator[dict[str, Any], None]:
                    async for msg in prompt:
                        collector.append(msg)
                        yield msg

                if args:
                    args = (_gen_wrapper(),) + args[1:]
                else:
                    kwargs["prompt"] = _gen_wrapper()
            else:
                self._prompt = str(prompt)

            return await super().query(*args, **kwargs)

        def _handle_assistant_tool_uses(
            self,
            msg: Any,
            run: Any,
            subagent_sessions: dict[str, Any],
        ) -> None:
            """Process tool uses for an assistant message."""
            if not hasattr(msg, "content"):
                return

            from ._hooks import _client_managed_runs

            parent_tool_use_id = getattr(msg, "parent_tool_use_id", None)

            for block in msg.content:
                if type(block).__name__ != "ToolUseBlock":
                    continue

                try:
                    tool_use_id = getattr(block, "id", None)
                    tool_name = getattr(block, "name", "unknown_tool")
                    tool_input = getattr(block, "input", {})

                    if not tool_use_id:
                        continue

                    start_time = time.time()

                    # Check if this is a Task tool (subagent)
                    if tool_name == "Task" and not parent_tool_use_id:
