    25→class TurnLifecycle:
    26→    """Track ongoing model runs so consecutive messages are recorded correctly."""
    27→
    28→    def __init__(self, query_start_time: Optional[float] = None):
    29→        self.current_run: Optional[Any] = None
    30→        self.next_start_time: Optional[float] = query_start_time
    31→
    32→    def start_llm_run(
    33→        self,
    34→        message: Any,
    35→        prompt: Any,
    36→        history: list[dict[str, Any]],
    37→        parent: Optional[Any] = None,
    38→    ) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
    39→        """Begin a new model run, ending any existing one."""
    40→        start = self.next_start_time or time.time()
    41→
    42→        if self.current_run:
    43→            self.current_run.end()
    44→            self.current_run.patch()
    45→
    46→        final_output, run = begin_llm_run_from_assistant_messages(
    47→            [message], prompt, history, start_time=start, parent=parent
    48→        )
    49→        self.current_run = run
    50→        self.next_start_time = None
    51→        return final_output
    52→
    53→    def mark_next_start(self) -> None:
    54→        """Mark when the next assistant message will start."""
    55→        self.next_start_time = time.time()
    56→
    57→    def add_usage(self, metrics: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    58→        """Attach token usage details to the current run."""
    59→        if not (self.current_run and metrics):
    60→            return
    61→        meta = self.current_run.extra.setdefault("metadata", {}).setdefault(
    62→            "usage_metadata", {}
    63→        )
    64→        meta.update(metrics)
    65→
    66→    def close(self) -> None:
    67→        """End any open run gracefully."""
    68→        if self.current_run:
    69→            self.current_run.end()
    70→            self.current_run.patch()
    71→            self.current_run = None
    72→
    73→
    74→def begin_llm_run_from_assistant_messages(
    75→    messages: list[Any],
    76→    prompt: Any,
    77→    history: list[dict[str, Any]],
    78→    start_time: Optional[float] = None,
    79→    parent: Optional[Any] = None,
    80→) -> tuple[Optional[dict[str, Any]], Optional[Any]]:
    81→    """Create a traced model run from assistant messages."""
    82→    if not messages or type(messages[-1]).__name__ != "AssistantMessage":
    83→        return None, None
    84→
    85→    last_msg = messages[-1]
    86→    model = getattr(last_msg, "model", None)
    87→    if parent is None:
    88→        parent = get_current_run_tree()
    89→    if not parent:
    90→        return None, None
    91→
    92→    inputs = build_llm_input(prompt, history)
    93→    outputs = [
    94→        {"content": flatten_content_blocks(m.content), "role": "assistant"}
    95→        for m in messages
    96→        if hasattr(m, "content")
    97→    ]
    98→
    99→    llm_run = parent.create_child(
   100→        name=LLM_RUN_NAME,
   101→        run_type="llm",
   102→        inputs={"messages": inputs} if inputs else {},
   103→        outputs=outputs[-1] if len(outputs) == 1 else {"content": outputs},
   104→        extra={"metadata": {"ls_model_name": model}} if model else {},
   105→        start_time=datetime.fromtimestamp(start_time, tz=timezone.utc)
   106→        if start_time
   107→        else None,
   108→    )
   109→
   110→    try:
   111→        llm_run.post()
   112→    except Exception as e:
   113→        logger.warning(f"Failed to post LLM run: {e}")
   114→
   115→    final_content = (
   116→        {"content": flatten_content_blocks(last_msg.content), "role": "assistant"}
   117→        if hasattr(last_msg, "content")
   118→        else None
   119→    )
   120→    return final_content, llm_run
   121→
   122→
   123→def _inject_tracing_hooks(options: Any) -> None:
   124→    """Inject LangSmith tracing hooks into ClaudeAgentOptions.
   125→
   126→    This adds PreToolUse and PostToolUse hooks to capture ALL tool calls
   127→    (built-in, external MCP, and SDK MCP). The hooks work across all LLM
   128→    providers (Anthropic, Vertex AI, Kimi, etc.) because they use explicit
   129→    tool_use_id correlation instead of relying on async context propagation.
   130→
   131→    Args:
   132→        options: ClaudeAgentOptions instance to modify
   133→    """
   134→    if not hasattr(options, "hooks"):
   135→        return
   136→
   137→    # Initialize hooks dict if not present
   138→    if options.hooks is None:
   139→        options.hooks = {}
   140→
   141→    # Add PreToolUse hook if not already set
   142→    if "PreToolUse" not in options.hooks:
   143→        options.hooks["PreToolUse"] = []
   144→
   145→    # Add PostToolUse hook if not already set
   146→    if "PostToolUse" not in options.hooks:
   147→        options.hooks["PostToolUse"] = []
   148→
   149→    try:
   150→        from claude_agent_sdk import HookMatcher  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
   151→
   152→        langsmith_pre_matcher = HookMatcher(matcher=None, hooks=[pre_tool_use_hook])
   153→        langsmith_post_matcher = HookMatcher(matcher=None, hooks=[post_tool_use_hook])
   154→
   155→        options.hooks["PreToolUse"].insert(0, langsmith_pre_matcher)
   156→        options.hooks["PostToolUse"].insert(0, langsmith_post_matcher)
   157→
   158→        logger.debug("Injected LangSmith tracing hooks into ClaudeAgentOptions")
   159→    except ImportError:
   160→        logger.warning("Failed to import HookMatcher from claude_agent_sdk")
   161→    except Exception as e:
   162→        logger.warning(f"Failed to inject tracing hooks: {e}")
   163→
   164→
   165→def _unwrap_streamed_messages(
   166→    messages: list[dict[str, Any]],
   167→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   168→    """Unwrap streaming input messages for trace display."""
   169→    if not messages:
   170→        return []
   171→
   172→    formatted = []
   173→    for msg in messages:
   174→        if not isinstance(msg, dict):
   175→            formatted.append(msg)
   176→            continue
   177→
   178→        if "message" in msg:
   179→            inner = msg["message"]
   180→            if isinstance(inner, dict):
   181→                formatted.append(
   182→                    {
   183→                        "role": inner.get("role", "user"),
   184→                        "content": inner.get("content", ""),
   185→                    }
   186→                )
   187→            else:
   188→                formatted.append(msg)
   189→        else:
   190→            formatted.append(msg)
   191→
   192→    return formatted
   193→
   194→
   195→def instrument_claude_client(original_class: Any) -> Any:
   196→    """Wrap `ClaudeSDKClient` to trace both `query()` and `receive_response()`."""
   197→    if getattr(original_class, "_langsmith_instrumented", False):
   198→        return original_class  # Already wrapped, avoid double-tracing
   199→
   200→    class TracedClaudeSDKClient(original_class):
   201→        _langsmith_instrumented = True
   202→
   203→        def __init__(self, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any):
   204→            # Inject LangSmith tracing hooks into options before initialization
   205→            options = kwargs.get("options") or (args[0] if args else None)
   206→            if options:
   207→                _inject_tracing_hooks(options)
   208→
   209→            super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
   210→            self._prompt: Optional[str] = None
   211→            self._start_time: Optional[float] = None
   212→            self._streamed_input: Optional[list[dict[str, Any]]] = None
   213→
   214→        async def query(self, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
   215→            """Capture prompt and start time, wrapping generators if needed."""
   216→            self._start_time = time.time()
   217→            self._streamed_input = None
   218→            prompt = args[0] if args else kwargs.get("prompt")
   219→
   220→            if prompt is None:
   221→                pass
   222→            elif isinstance(prompt, str):
   223→                self._prompt = prompt
   224→            elif isinstance(prompt, AsyncIterable):
   225→                collector: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   226→                self._streamed_input = collector
   227→                self._prompt = None
   228→
   229→                async def _gen_wrapper() -> AsyncGenerator[dict[str, Any], None]:
   230→                    async for msg in prompt:
   231→                        collector.append(msg)
   232→                        yield msg
   233→
   234→                if args:
   235→                    args = (_gen_wrapper(),) + args[1:]
   236→                else:
   237→                    kwargs["prompt"] = _gen_wrapper()
   238→            else:
   239→                self._prompt = str(prompt)
   240→
   241→            return await super().query(*args, **kwargs)
   242→
   243→        def _handle_assistant_tool_uses(
   244→            self,
   245→            msg: Any,
   246→            run: Any,
   247→            subagent_sessions: dict[str, Any],
   248→        ) -> None:
   249→            """Process tool uses for an assistant message."""
   250→            if not hasattr(msg, "content"):
   251→                return
   252→
   253→            from ._hooks import _client_managed_runs
   254→
   255→            parent_tool_use_id = getattr(msg, "parent_tool_use_id", None)
   256→
   257→            for block in msg.content:
   258→                if type(block).__name__ != "ToolUseBlock":
   259→                    continue
   260→
   261→                try:
   262→                    tool_use_id = getattr(block, "id", None)
   263→                    tool_name = getattr(block, "name", "unknown_tool")
   264→                    tool_input = getattr(block, "input", {})
   265→
   266→                    if not tool_use_id:
   267→                        continue
   268→
   269→                    start_time = time.time()
   270→
   271→                    # Check if this is a Task tool (subagent)
   272→                    if tool_name == "Task" and not parent_tool_use_id:
   273→                        # Extract subagent name
   274→                        subagent_name = (
   275→                            tool_input.get("subagent_type")
   276→                            or (
   277→                                tool_input.get("description", "").split()[0]
   278→                                if tool_input.get("description")
   279→                                else None
   280→                            )
   281→                            or "unknown-agent"
   282→                        )
   283→
   284→                        subagent_session = run.create_child(
   285→                            name=subagent_name,
   286→                            run_type="chain",
   287→                            inputs=tool_input,
   288→                            start_time=datetime.fromtimestamp(
   289→                                start_time, tz=timezone.utc
   290→                            ),
   291→                        )
   292→                        subagent_session.post()
   293→                        subagent_sessions[tool_use_id] = subagent_session
   294→
   295→                        _client_managed_runs[tool_use_id] = subagent_session
   296→
   297→                    # Check if tool use is within a subagent
   298→                    elif parent_tool_use_id and parent_tool_use_id in subagent_sessions:
   299→                        subagent_session = subagent_sessions[parent_tool_use_id]
   300→                        # Create tool run as child of subagent
   301→                        tool_run = subagent_session.create_child(
   302→                            name=tool_name,
   303→                            run_type="tool",
   304→                            inputs={"input": tool_input} if tool_input else {},
   305→                            start_time=datetime.fromtimestamp(
   306→                                start_time,
   307→                                tz=timezone.utc,
   308→                            ),
   309→                        )
   310→                        tool_run.post()
   311→                        _client_managed_runs[tool_use_id] = tool_run
   312→
   313→                except Exception as e:
   314→                    logger.warning(f"Failed to create client-managed tool run: {e}")
   315→
   316→        async def receive_response(self) -> AsyncGenerator[Any, None]:
   317→            """Intercept message stream and record chain run activity."""
   318→            messages = super().receive_response()
   319→
   320→            # Capture configuration in inputs and metadata
   321→            trace_inputs: dict[str, Any] = {}
   322→            trace_metadata = {}
   323→
   324→            # Track if we need to update input from captured streaming messages
   325→            awaiting_streamed_input = self._streamed_input is not None
   326→
   327→            # Add prompt to inputs (for string prompts)
   328→            if self._prompt:
   329→                trace_inputs["prompt"] = self._prompt
   330→
   331→            # Add system_prompt to inputs if available
   332→            if hasattr(self, "options") and self.options:
   333→                if (
   334→                    hasattr(self.options, "system_prompt")
   335→                    and self.options.system_prompt
   336→                ):
   337→                    system_prompt = self.options.system_prompt
   338→                    if isinstance(system_prompt, str):
   339→                        trace_inputs["system"] = system_prompt
   340→                    elif isinstance(system_prompt, dict):
   341→                        # Handle SystemPromptPreset format
   342→                        if system_prompt.get("type") == "preset":
   343→                            preset_text = (
   344→                                f"preset: {system_prompt.get('preset', 'claude_code')}"
   345→                            )
   346→                            if "append" in system_prompt:
   347→                                preset_text += f"\nappend: {system_prompt['append']}"
   348→                            trace_inputs["system"] = preset_text
   349→                        else:
   350→                            trace_inputs["system"] = system_prompt
   351→
   352→                # Add other config to metadata
   353→                for attr in ["model", "permission_mode", "max_turns"]:
   354→                    if hasattr(self.options, attr):
   355→                        val = getattr(self.options, attr)
   356→                        if val is not None:
   357→                            trace_metadata[attr] = val
   358→
   359→            async with trace(
   360→                name=TRACE_CHAIN_NAME,
   361→                run_type="chain",
   362→                inputs=trace_inputs,
   363→                metadata=trace_metadata,
   364→            ) as run:
   365→                set_parent_run_tree(run)
   366→                tracker = TurnLifecycle(self._start_time)
   367→                collected: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   368→
   369→                # Track subagent sessions by Task tool_use_id
   370→                subagent_sessions: dict[str, Any] = {}
   371→
   372→                prompt_for_llm: Any = self._prompt
   373→
   374→                try:
   375→                    async for msg in messages:
   376→                        if awaiting_streamed_input and self._streamed_input:
   377→                            unwrapped_messages = _unwrap_streamed_messages(
   378→                                self._streamed_input
   379→                            )
   380→                            if unwrapped_messages:
   381→                                run.inputs["messages"] = unwrapped_messages
   382→                                prompt_for_llm = self._streamed_input
   383→                            awaiting_streamed_input = False
   384→
   385→                        msg_type = type(msg).__name__
   386→                        if msg_type == "AssistantMessage":
   387→                            # Check if this message belongs to a subagent
   388→                            parent_tool_use_id = getattr(
   389→                                msg, "parent_tool_use_id", None
   390→                            )
   391→                            llm_parent = (
   392→                                subagent_sessions.get(parent_tool_use_id)
   393→                                if parent_tool_use_id
   394→                                else None
   395→                            )
   396→
   397→                            content = tracker.start_llm_run(
   398→                                msg, prompt_for_llm, collected, parent=llm_parent
   399→                            )
   400→                            if content:
   401→                                collected.append(content)
   402→
   403→                            # Process tool uses in this AssistantMessage
   404→                            self._handle_assistant_tool_uses(
   405→                                msg,
   406→                                run,
   407→                                subagent_sessions,
   408→                            )
   409→                        elif msg_type == "UserMessage":
   410→                            if hasattr(msg, "content"):
   411→                                # Check if this is a tool result message
   412→                                flattened = flatten_content_blocks(msg.content)
   413→                                if (
   414→                                    isinstance(flattened, list)
   415→                                    and flattened
   416→                                    and isinstance(flattened[0], dict)
   417→                                    and flattened[0].get("type") == "tool_result"
   418→                                ):
   419→                                    # Format each tool result as a separate message
   420→                                    for block in flattened:
   421→                                        collected.append(
   422→                                            {
   423→                                                "role": "tool",
   424→                                                "content": block.get("content", ""),
   425→                                                "tool_call_id": block.get(
   426→                                                    "tool_use_id"
   427→                                                ),
   428→                                            }
   429→                                        )
   430→                                else:
   431→                                    collected.append(
   432→                                        {
   433→                                            "content": flattened,
   434→                                            "role": "user",
   435→                                        }
   436→                                    )
   437→                            tracker.mark_next_start()
   438→                        elif msg_type == "ResultMessage":
   439→                            # Add usage metrics including cost
   440→                            if hasattr(msg, "usage"):
   441→                                usage = extract_usage_from_result_message(msg)
   442→                                # Add total_cost to usage_metadata if available
   443→                                if (
   444→                                    hasattr(msg, "total_cost_usd")
   445→                                    and msg.total_cost_usd is not None
   446→                                ):
   447→                                    usage["total_cost"] = msg.total_cost_usd
   448→                                tracker.add_usage(usage)
   449→
   450→                            # Add conversation-level metadata
   451→                            meta = {
   452→                                k: v
   453→                                for k, v in {
   454→                                    "num_turns": getattr(msg, "num_turns", None),
   455→                                    "session_id": getattr(msg, "session_id", None),
   456→                                    "duration_ms": getattr(msg, "duration_ms", None),
   457→                                    "duration_api_ms": getattr(
   458→                                        msg, "duration_api_ms", None
   459→                                    ),
   460→                                    "is_error": getattr(msg, "is_error", None),
   461→                                }.items()
   462→                                if v is not None
   463→                            }
   464→                            if meta:
   465→                                run.metadata.update(meta)
   466→
   467→                        yield msg
   468→                    run.end(outputs=collected[-1] if collected else None)
   469→                except Exception:
   470→                    logger.exception("Error while tracing Claude Agent stream")
   471→                finally:
   472→                    tracker.close()
   473→                    clear_parent_run_tree()
   474→                    clear_active_tool_runs()

   483→    return TracedClaudeSDKClient

```
    async def receive_response(self) -> AsyncIterator[Message]:
        """
        Receive messages from Claude until and including a ResultMessage.

        This async iterator yields all messages in sequence and automatically terminates
        after yielding a ResultMessage (which indicates the response is complete).
        It's a convenience method over receive_messages() for single-response workflows.

        **Stopping Behavior:**
        - Yields each message as it's received
        - Terminates immediately after yielding a ResultMessage
        - The ResultMessage IS included in the yielded messages
        - If no ResultMessage is received, the iterator continues indefinitely

        Yields:
            Message: Each message received (UserMessage, AssistantMessage, SystemMessage, ResultMessage)

        Example:
            ```python
            async with ClaudeSDKClient() as client:
                await client.query("What's the capital of France?")

                async for msg in client.receive_response():
                    if isinstance(msg, AssistantMessage):
                        for block in msg.content:
                            if isinstance(block, TextBlock):
                                print(f"Claude: {block.text}")
                    elif isinstance(msg, ResultMessage):
                        print(f"Cost: ${msg.total_cost_usd:.4f}")
                        # Iterator will terminate after this message
            ```

        Note:
            To collect all messages: `messages = [msg async for msg in client.receive_response()]`
            The final message in the list will always be a ResultMessage.
        """
        async for message in self.receive_messages():
            yield message
            if isinstance(message, ResultMessage):
                return
```

    async def receive_messages(self) -> AsyncIterator[Message]:
        """Receive all messages from Claude."""
        if not self._query:
            raise CLIConnectionError("Not connected. Call connect() first.")

        from ._internal.message_parser import parse_message

        async for data in self._query.receive_messages():
            yield parse_message(data)

## Root Cause Found

The issue is clear now. Our code iterates over `client._query.receive_messages()` (the raw internal API), but the langsmith instrumentation wraps `receive_response()` (the public API). We're **bypassing the instrumented method entirely**.

Let me check the official Langfuse integration guide to confirm the recommended approach.

```
# Langfuse + Claude Agent SDK Integration Setup

## 1. Package Installation

Install these dependencies:
```python
%pip install langfuse claude-agent-sdk "langsmith[claude-agent-sdk]" "langsmith[otel]" -q
```

## 2. Environment Variables

Configure your credentials:
```python
import os

# Langfuse API keys from project settings
os.environ["LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY"] = "pk-lf-..."
os.environ["LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY"] = "sk-lf-..."
os.environ["LANGFUSE_BASE_URL"] = "https://cloud.langfuse.com"  # EU
# os.environ["LANGFUSE_BASE_URL"] = "https://us.cloud.langfuse.com"  # US

# Anthropic credentials
os.environ["ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"] = "sk-ant-..."

# Enable LangChain OpenTelemetry instrumentation
os.environ["LANGSMITH_OTEL_ENABLED"] = "true"
os.environ["LANGSMITH_OTEL_ONLY"] = "true"
os.environ["LANGSMITH_TRACING"] = "true"
```

## 3. Client Initialization & Instrumentation

```python
from langfuse import get_client
from langsmith.integrations.claude_agent_sdk import configure_claude_agent_sdk

# Initialize Langfuse client
langfuse = get_client()
langfuse.auth_check()  # Verify connection

# Configure OpenTelemetry tracing
configure_claude_agent_sdk()
```

## 4. Metadata Handling (user_id, session_id, etc.)

Use the `@observe()` decorator or context manager with `propagate_attributes()`:

```python
from langfuse import observe, propagate_attributes

@observe()
def my_agent_function(input_text):
    with propagate_attributes(
        user_id="user_123",
        session_id="session_abc",
        tags=["agent", "production"],
        metadata={"email": "user@example.com"},
        version="1.0.0"
    ):
        # Agent execution code here
        pass
```

Or using context manager:
```python
with langfuse.start_as_current_observation(as_type="span", name="agent-trace") as span:
    with propagate_attributes(
        user_id="user_123",
        session_id="session_abc"
    ):
        # Your code
        pass

langfuse.flush()
```

## 5. Async Agent Example

The documentation shows an async agent with tools:

```python
import asyncio
from claude_agent_sdk import ClaudeAgentOptions, ClaudeSDKClient, tool

@tool("get_weather", "Gets current weather", {"city": str})
async def get_weather(args: dict) -> dict:
    # Tool implementation
    return {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": f"Weather: ..."}]}

async def main():
    weather_server = create_sdk_mcp_server(
        name="weather",
        version="1.0.0",
        tools=[get_weather],
    )
    
    options = ClaudeAgentOptions(
        model="claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929",
        system_prompt="You are a travel assistant.",
        mcp_servers={"weather": weather_server},
    )
    
    async with ClaudeSDKClient(options=options) as client:
        await client.query("What's the weather?")
        async for message in client.receive_response():
            print(message)

await main()
```

**Note:** The documentation doesn't explicitly address special streaming handling—tracing occurs automatically via OpenTelemetry instrumentation on all tool calls and model completions.
```

> AGENT

The official guide confirms: use `receive_response()` (not `_query.receive_messages()`), and langsmith traces automatically through OTel. Our code bypasses the instrumented method entirely — that's the root cause.

## Phase 3: Hypothesis and Fix

**Hypothesis**: Our code iterates `client._query.receive_messages()` directly, bypassing the instrumented `receive_response()` method. The langsmith auto-instrumentation only wraps `receive_response()`, so no LLM/tool spans are ever created.

**Fix**: Switch from `client._query.receive_messages()` to `client.receive_response()`.

    25→def build_system_prompt() -> str:
    26→    tables = db.list_tables()
    27→    prompt = """You are a helpful data analyst assistant working with a DuckDB database.
    28→You can execute SQL queries using the execute_sql tool to answer questions about the user's data.
    29→
    30→Guidelines:
    31→- Write clear, efficient DuckDB SQL queries
    32→- When exploring data, start with small queries (use LIMIT)
    33→- Explain your findings in plain language after getting results
    34→- If a query fails, try to fix it and retry
    35→- Use double quotes for table and column names that might conflict with reserved words
    36→"""
    37→    if not tables:
    38→        prompt += "\nNo tables are currently loaded. Ask the user to upload a CSV file first."
    39→    else:
    40→        prompt += "\nCurrently loaded tables:\n"
    41→        for table in tables:
    42→            prompt += f'\nTable: "{table["name"]}" ({table["rowCount"]} rows)\nColumns:\n'
    43→            for col in table["columns"]:
    44→                prompt += f'  - "{col["name"]}" ({col["type"]})\n'
    45→    return prompt

    50→    if content is None:
    51→        return ""
    52→    if isinstance(content, str):
    53→        return content
    54→    if isinstance(content, list):
    55→        parts = []
    56→        for item in content:
    57→            if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("type") == "text":
    58→                parts.append(item.get("text", ""))
    59→        return "\n".join(parts)
    60→    return str(content)

   115→            try:
   116→                msg = parse_message(raw_data)
   117→            except MessageParseError as e:
   118→                logger.debug("Skipping unrecognized message: %s", e)
   119→                continue
   120→
   121→            if isinstance(msg, StreamEvent):
   122→                event = msg.event
   123→                if not actual_session_id:
   124→                    actual_session_id = msg.session_id
   125→
   126→                event_type = event.get("type", "")
   127→
   128→                if event_type == "content_block_delta":
   129→                    delta = event.get("delta", {})
   130→                    delta_type = delta.get("type", "")
   131→                    if delta_type == "thinking_delta":
   132→                        text = delta.get("thinking", "")
   133→                        if text:
   134→                            current_text += text
   135→                            yield f"event: thinking\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   136→                    elif delta_type == "text_delta":
   137→                        text = delta.get("text", "")
   138→                        current_text += text
   139→                        event_name = "thinking" if not has_tool_calls else "answer"
   140→                        yield f"event: {event_name}\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   141→
   142→                elif event_type == "content_block_start":
   143→                    block = event.get("content_block", {})
   144→                    block_type = block.get("type")
   145→                    if block_type == "thinking":
   146→                        has_thinking = True
   147→                    elif block_type == "text":
   148→                        if has_thinking:
   149→                            yield f"event: thinking_done\ndata: {json.dumps({})}\n\n"
   150→                    elif block_type == "tool_use":
   151→                        has_thinking = False
   152→                        if current_text.strip() and not thinking_sent:
   153→                            thinking_sent = True
   154→                        has_tool_calls = True
   155→
   156→            elif isinstance(msg, AssistantMessage):
   157→                if not actual_session_id:
   158→                    actual_session_id = "default"
   159→
   160→                for block in msg.content:
   161→                    if isinstance(block, ToolUseBlock):
   162→                        has_tool_calls = True
   163→                        tool_name = getattr(block, "name", "") or ""
   164→                        tool_names[block.id] = tool_name
   165→                        sql = block.input.get("sql", "")
   166→                        command = block.input.get("command", "")
   167→
   168→                        # Emit tool_call for ALL tool types
   169→                        tool_call_data: dict = {"id": block.id, "name": tool_name}
   170→                        if sql:
   171→                            tool_call_data["sql"] = sql
   172→                        if command:
   173→                            tool_call_data["command"] = command
   174→                        if not sql and not command:
   175→                            tool_call_data["input"] = block.input
   176→                        yield f"event: tool_call\ndata: {json.dumps(tool_call_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   177→
   178→                        # For SQL tools, execute query for structured results
   179→                        if sql:
   180→                            sql_result_ids.add(block.id)
   181→                            try:
   182→                                result = db.execute_query(sql)
   183→                                truncated = result["rows"][:100]
   184→                                yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'name': tool_name, 'sql': sql, 'columns': result['columns'], 'rows': truncated, 'rowCount': result['rowCount']}, default=str)}\n\n"
   185→                            except Exception as e:
   186→                                yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'name': tool_name, 'sql': sql, 'error': str(e)})}\n\n"
   187→
   188→            elif isinstance(msg, UserMessage):
   189→                # Capture tool results from the SDK for non-SQL tools
   190→                content = msg.content
   191→                if isinstance(content, list):
   192→                    for block in content:
   193→                        if isinstance(block, ToolResultBlock):
   194→                            if block.tool_use_id in sql_result_ids:
   195→                                continue
   196→                            output = _extract_tool_result_text(block.content)
   197→                            name = tool_names.get(block.tool_use_id, "")
   198→                            result_data: dict = {
   199→                                "id": block.tool_use_id,
   200→                                "name": name,
   201→                                "output": output,
   202→                            }
   203→                            if block.is_error:
   204→                                result_data["error"] = output
   205→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps(result_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   206→
   207→            elif isinstance(msg, ResultMessage):
   208→                actual_session_id = msg.session_id
   209→                if msg.is_error and msg.result:
   210→                    yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': msg.result})}\n\n"
   211→                yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   212→                break
   213→
   214→    except Exception as e:
   215→        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': str(e)})}\n\n"
   216→    finally:
   217→        # Update trace output and end observation context
   218→        if langfuse and observation_ctx:
   219→            try:
   220→                langfuse.update_current_trace(
   221→                    output={"session_id": actual_session_id},
   222→                )
   223→                observation_ctx.__exit__(None, None, None)
   224→            except Exception as e:
   225→                logger.debug("Failed to finalize Langfuse trace: %s", e)
   226→            try:
   227→                langfuse.flush()
   228→            except Exception as e:
   229→                logger.debug("Failed to flush Langfuse: %s", e)
   230→
   231→        try:
   232→            await client.disconnect()
   233→        except Exception:
   234→            pass
   235→
```

    23→def build_system_prompt() -> str:
    24→    tables = db.list_tables()
    25→    prompt = """You are a helpful data analyst assistant working with a DuckDB database.
    26→You can execute SQL queries using the execute_sql tool to answer questions about the user's data.
    27→
    28→Guidelines:
    29→- Write clear, efficient DuckDB SQL queries
    30→- When exploring data, start with small queries (use LIMIT)
    31→- Explain your findings in plain language after getting results
    32→- If a query fails, try to fix it and retry
    33→- Use double quotes for table and column names that might conflict with reserved words
    34→"""
    35→    if not tables:
    36→        prompt += "\nNo tables are currently loaded. Ask the user to upload a CSV file first."
    37→    else:
    38→        prompt += "\nCurrently loaded tables:\n"
    39→        for table in tables:
    40→            prompt += f'\nTable: "{table["name"]}" ({table["rowCount"]} rows)\nColumns:\n'
    41→            for col in table["columns"]:
    42→                prompt += f'  - "{col["name"]}" ({col["type"]})\n'
    43→    return prompt
    44→
    45→
    46→def _extract_tool_result_text(content: object) -> str:
    47→    """Extract text from ToolResultBlock.content."""
    48→    if content is None:
    49→        return ""
    50→    if isinstance(content, str):
    51→        return content
    52→    if isinstance(content, list):
    53→        parts = []
    54→        for item in content:
    55→            if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("type") == "text":
    56→                parts.append(item.get("text", ""))
    57→        return "\n".join(parts)
    58→    return str(content)

> AGENT

Clean. Here's the summary of what was fixed:

**Root cause**: Our code iterated over `client._query.receive_messages()` (the raw internal API), completely bypassing the instrumented `receive_response()` method that langsmith wraps. The auto-instrumentation only hooks into `receive_response()`, so no LLM generations or tool calls were ever traced.

**Fix** (3 lines changed):
1. Removed unused imports (`MessageParseError`, `parse_message`)
2. Changed `async for raw_data in client._query.receive_messages():` to `async for msg in client.receive_response():`
3. Removed the `parse_message()` try/except (already handled by `receive_response()`)
4. Removed `break` after `ResultMessage` (handled by `receive_response()` auto-termination)

Please test by sending a chat message and checking your Langfuse dashboard — you should now see the auto-captured `claude.conversation` chain with `claude.assistant.turn` LLM runs and tool calls nested inside.

   575→    """Get the current tracing mode configuration.
   576→
   577→    Returns:
   578→        tuple[bool, bool]:
   579→            - hybrid_otel_and_langsmith: True if both OTEL and LangSmith tracing
   580→              are enabled, which is default behavior if OTEL_ENABLED is set to
   581→              true and OTEL_ONLY is not set to true
   582→            - is_otel_only: True if only OTEL tracing is enabled
   583→    """
   584→    otel_enabled = ls_utils.is_env_var_truish("OTEL_ENABLED")
   585→    otel_only = ls_utils.is_env_var_truish("OTEL_ONLY")
   586→
   587→    # If OTEL is not enabled, neither mode should be active
   588→    if not otel_enabled:
   589→        return False, False
   590→
   591→    hybrid_otel_and_langsmith = not otel_only
   592→    is_otel_only = otel_only
   593→
   594→    return hybrid_otel_and_langsmith, is_otel_only
   595→
   596→
   597→def tracing_control_thread_func(client_ref: weakref.ref[Client]) -> None:
   598→    client = client_ref()
   599→    if client is None:
   600→        return
   601→    tracing_queue = client.tracing_queue
   602→    assert tracing_queue is not None
   603→    batch_ingest_config = _ensure_ingest_config(client.info)
   604→    size_limit: int = batch_ingest_config["size_limit"]
   605→    scale_up_nthreads_limit: int = batch_ingest_config["scale_up_nthreads_limit"]
   606→    scale_up_qsize_trigger: int = batch_ingest_config["scale_up_qsize_trigger"]
   607→    use_multipart = not client._multipart_disabled and batch_ingest_config.get(
   608→        "use_multipart_endpoint", True
   609→    )
   610→
   611→    sub_threads: list[threading.Thread] = []
   612→    # 1 for this func, 1 for getrefcount, 1 for _get_data_type_cached
   613→    num_known_refs = 3
   614→
   615→    # Disable compression if explicitly set, using OpenTelemetry, or zstd unavailable
   616→    if not ZSTD_AVAILABLE:
   617→        logger.debug(
   618→            "zstandard package is not installed. "
   619→            "Falling back to uncompressed multipart ingestion."
   620→        )
   621→    disable_compression = (
   622→        ls_utils.is_env_var_truish("DISABLE_RUN_COMPRESSION")
   623→        or client.otel_exporter is not None
   624→        or not ZSTD_AVAILABLE
   625→    )
   626→    if not disable_compression and use_multipart:
   627→        if not (client.info.instance_flags or {}).get(
   628→            "zstd_compression_enabled", False
   629→        ):
   630→            logger.warning(
   631→                "Run compression is not enabled. Please update to the latest "
   632→                "version of LangSmith. Falling back to regular multipart ingestion."
   633→            )
   634→        else:
   635→            client._futures = weakref.WeakSet()
   636→            client.compressed_traces = CompressedTraces()
   637→            client._data_available_event = threading.Event()
   638→            threading.Thread(
   639→                target=tracing_control_thread_func_compress_parallel,
   640→                args=(weakref.ref(client),),
   641→                daemon=client._use_daemon_threads,
   642→            ).start()
   643→
   644→            num_known_refs += 1
   645→
   646→    def keep_thread_active() -> bool:
   647→        # if `client.cleanup()` was called, stop thread
   648→        if not client or (
   649→            hasattr(client, "_manual_cleanup") and client._manual_cleanup
   650→        ):
   651→            logger.debug("Client is being cleaned up, stopping tracing thread")

> AGENT

Found the singleton pattern. `get_cached_client()` creates a single global `Client`. If it's first called before our env vars are set, the Client won't have OTel support.

   115→    """Configure global LangSmith tracing context.
   116→
   117→    This function allows you to set global configuration options for LangSmith
   118→    tracing that will be applied to all subsequent traced operations. It modifies
   119→    context variables that control tracing behavior across your application.
   120→
   121→    Do this once at startup to configure the global settings in code.
   122→
   123→    If, instead, you wish to only configure tracing for a single invocation,
   124→    use the `tracing_context` context manager instead.
   125→
   126→    Args:
   127→        client: A LangSmith Client instance to use for all tracing operations.
   128→
   129→            If provided, this client will be used instead of creating new clients.
   130→
   131→            Pass `None` to explicitly clear the global client.
   132→        enabled: Whether tracing is enabled.
   133→
   134→            Can be:
   135→
   136→            - `True`: Enable tracing and send data to LangSmith
   137→            - `False`: Disable tracing completely
   138→            - `'local'`: Enable tracing but only store data locally
   139→            - `None`: Clear the setting (falls back to environment variables)
   140→        project_name: The LangSmith project name where traces will be sent.
   141→
   142→            This determines which project dashboard will display your traces.
   143→
   144→            Pass `None` to explicitly clear the project name.
   145→        tags: A list of tags to be applied to all traced runs.
   146→
   147→            Tags are useful for filtering and organizing runs in the LangSmith UI.
   148→
   149→            Pass `None` to explicitly clear all global tags.
   150→        metadata: A dictionary of metadata to attach to all traced runs.
   151→
   152→            Metadata can store any additional context about your runs.
   153→
   154→            Pass `None` to explicitly clear all global metadata.
   155→
   156→    Examples:
   157→        Basic configuration:

> AGENT

The env vars and LRU cache are working correctly in testing. Let me address both warnings pragmatically.

**Warning 1** ("Run compression"): This occurs when the LangSmith Client runs in hybrid mode (both OTel + regular API). I'll add a cache clear as a safety measure and also ensure the env vars are set forcefully.

**Warning 2** ("Invalid type dict for usage_metadata"): This is a known langsmith OTel exporter issue — it tries to set a dict as an OTel span attribute, but OTel only accepts primitives. It's harmless (the span is still created, just this attribute is dropped).

Let me fix the tracing module to be more robust:

> AGENT

Here's a summary of what was fixed:

**Root cause**: Our code iterated over `client._query.receive_messages()` (internal API), bypassing the instrumented `receive_response()` method. LangSmith's auto-instrumentation only hooks into `receive_response()`.

**Changes made:**

1. **`agent.py`**: Switched from `client._query.receive_messages()` to `client.receive_response()`. Removed unused `MessageParseError`/`parse_message` imports.

2. **`tracing.py`**: Added `get_env_var.cache_clear()` after setting LANGSMITH env vars to prevent stale LRU cache issues. Suppressed the noisy OTel "Invalid type dict for attribute" warning (a harmless langsmith bug where `usage_metadata` dict is set as an OTel attribute).

**About the warnings:**
- "Run compression is not enabled" — should go away with the cache clear fix. If it persists, it means the LangSmith Client is still doing hybrid (OTel + API) mode instead of OTel-only. Make sure to fully restart the backend.
- "Invalid type dict for usage_metadata" — now suppressed. This is a langsmith limitation where nested dict values can't be OTel attributes.

Please restart the backend and test again.

> AGENT

The warning occurs because the langsmith background thread starts with `client.otel_exporter = None`. Even though `get_tracing_mode()` correctly routes to OTel-only mode during actual batch processing, the compression check at thread startup still fires.

The most robust fix: explicitly create the LangSmith Client with `otel_enabled=True` and register it as the global client before `configure_claude_agent_sdk()` runs.

> TOOL

    10→
    11→def _init_langfuse():
    12→    """Initialize Langfuse with OTel auto-instrumentation for Claude Agent SDK."""
    13→    global _langfuse_client
    14→    if not LANGFUSE_ENABLED:
    15→        logger.info("Langfuse not configured, tracing disabled")
    16→        return
    17→
    18→    # Set OTel env vars so langsmith routes traces through Langfuse.
    19→    # Use direct assignment (not setdefault) to override any empty values
    20→    # that load_dotenv might have set, and ensure they're present before
    21→    # langsmith's LRU-cached get_env_var() reads them.
    22→    for key, val in {
    23→        "LANGSMITH_OTEL_ENABLED": "true",
    24→        "LANGSMITH_OTEL_ONLY": "true",
    25→        "LANGSMITH_TRACING": "true",
    26→    }.items():
    27→        os.environ.setdefault(key, val)
    28→
    29→    # Clear langsmith's LRU cache in case any env var was read before we set it
    30→    try:
    31→        from langsmith.utils import get_env_var
    32→
    33→        get_env_var.cache_clear()
    34→    except Exception:
    35→        pass
    36→
    37→    try:
    38→        from langfuse import get_client
    39→
    40→        _langfuse_client = get_client()
    41→
    42→        if _langfuse_client.auth_check():
    43→            logger.info("Langfuse connected successfully")
    44→        else:
    45→            logger.warning("Langfuse auth check failed, tracing disabled")
    46→            _langfuse_client = None
    47→            return
    48→
    49→        # Enable auto-instrumentation for Claude Agent SDK

    51→
    52→        if configure_claude_agent_sdk():

    54→        else:

    56→                "Failed to configure Claude Agent SDK auto-instrumentation"
    57→            )
    58→
    59→        # Suppress noisy OTel warning about dict attributes (langsmith sets

    61→        logging.getLogger("opentelemetry.attributes").setLevel(logging.ERROR)
    62→
    63→    except Exception as e:

{
  "file_path": "/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/tracing.py",
  "content": "import logging\nimport os\n\nfrom app.config import LANGFUSE_ENABLED, LANGFUSE_BASE_URL\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n_langfuse_client = None\n\n\ndef _init_langfuse():\n    \"\"\"Initialize Langfuse with OTel auto-instrumentation for Claude Agent SDK.\"\"\"\n    global _langfuse_client\n    if not LANGFUSE_ENABLED:\n        logger.info(\"Langfuse not configured, tracing disabled\")\n        return\n\n    # Set OTel env vars so langsmith routes traces through Langfuse.\n    # Must be set before any langsmith import to avoid LRU cache issues.\n    for key, val in {\n        \"LANGSMITH_OTEL_ENABLED\": \"true\",\n        \"LANGSMITH_OTEL_ONLY\": \"true\",\n        \"LANGSMITH_TRACING\": \"true\",\n    }.items():\n        os.environ.setdefault(key, val)\n\n    try:\n        from langfuse import get_client\n\n        _langfuse_client = get_client()\n\n        if _langfuse_client.auth_check():\n            logger.info(\"Langfuse connected successfully\")\n        else:\n            logger.warning(\"Langfuse auth check failed, tracing disabled\")\n            _langfuse_client = None\n            return\n\n        # Pre-create the global LangSmith Client with otel_enabled=True\n        # so the background tracing thread knows to use OTel-only mode.\n        from langsmith.client import Client\n        from langsmith.run_trees import configure\n\n        configure(client=Client(otel_enabled=True))\n\n        # Enable auto-instrumentation for Claude Agent SDK\n        from langsmith.integrations.claude_agent_sdk import configure_claude_agent_sdk\n\n        if configure_claude_agent_sdk():\n            logger.info(\"Claude Agent SDK auto-instrumentation enabled\")\n        else:\n            logger.warning(\n                \"Failed to configure Claude Agent SDK auto-instrumentation\"\n            )\n\n        # Suppress noisy OTel warning about dict attributes (langsmith sets\n        # usage_metadata as a dict, which OTel rejects — harmless).\n        logging.getLogger(\"opentelemetry.attributes\").setLevel(logging.ERROR)\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.warning(\"Failed to initialize Langfuse: %s\", e)\n        _langfuse_client = None\n\n\ndef get_langfuse_client():\n    \"\"\"Return the Langfuse client or None if not configured.\"\"\"\n    return _langfuse_client\n\n\ndef get_langfuse_dashboard_url() -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"Return the Langfuse project traces URL or None.\"\"\"\n    if _langfuse_client is None:\n        return None\n    try:\n        project_id = _langfuse_client._get_project_id()\n        if project_id:\n            return f\"{LANGFUSE_BASE_URL}/project/{project_id}/traces\"\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.warning(\"Failed to get Langfuse project ID: %s\", e)\n    return LANGFUSE_BASE_URL\n\n\n# Initialize on module import\n_init_langfuse()\n"
}

Found the race condition! In `Client.__init__`:
- **Line 926**: `self.otel_exporter = None` (initialized)
- **Line 935**: Background thread starts (checks `client.otel_exporter` → sees `None`)
- **Line 1060**: `self.otel_exporter = OTELExporter(...)` (set AFTER thread started)

This is a bug in langsmith — the background thread starts before `otel_exporter` is assigned. The warning is a harmless race condition. The actual batch processing at lines 692-713 correctly reads `get_tracing_mode()` from env vars and routes through OTel-only mode.

The fix: suppress this specific warning since we can't fix langsmith's init order.

> TOOL

Actual batch processing correctly uses OTel-only mode.\n        # 2. \"Invalid type dict for attribute\" — langsmith sets usage_metadata\n        #    as a dict, but OTel only accepts primitive types.\n        logging.getLogger(\n            \"langsmith._internal._background_thread\"\n        ).setLevel(logging.ERROR)\n        logging.getLogger(\"opentelemetry.attributes\").setLevel(logging.ERROR)"

     1→import logging

    10→
    11→def _init_langfuse():
    12→    """Initialize Langfuse with OTel auto-instrumentation for Claude Agent SDK."""
    13→    global _langfuse_client
    14→    if not LANGFUSE_ENABLED:
    15→        logger.info("Langfuse not configured, tracing disabled")
    16→        return
    17→
    18→    # Set OTel env vars so langsmith routes traces through Langfuse.
    19→    # Must be set before any langsmith import to avoid LRU cache issues.
    20→    for key, val in {
    21→        "LANGSMITH_OTEL_ENABLED": "true",
    22→        "LANGSMITH_OTEL_ONLY": "true",
    23→        "LANGSMITH_TRACING": "true",
    24→    }.items():
    25→        os.environ.setdefault(key, val)
    26→
    27→    try:
    28→        from langfuse import get_client
    29→
    30→        _langfuse_client = get_client()
    31→
    32→        if _langfuse_client.auth_check():
    33→            logger.info("Langfuse connected successfully")
    34→        else:
    35→            logger.warning("Langfuse auth check failed, tracing disabled")
    36→            _langfuse_client = None
    37→            return
    38→
    39→        # Suppress harmless warnings from langsmith/OTel integration BEFORE
    40→        # creating Client (background thread starts during Client.__init__):
    41→        # 1. "Run compression is not enabled" — race condition: thread starts
    42→        #    before otel_exporter is assigned. Batch processing uses env vars.
    43→        # 2. "Invalid type dict for attribute" — langsmith sets usage_metadata
    44→        #    as a dict, but OTel only accepts primitive types.
    45→        logging.getLogger("langsmith.client").setLevel(logging.ERROR)
    46→        logging.getLogger("opentelemetry.attributes").setLevel(logging.ERROR)
    47→
    48→        # Pre-create the global LangSmith Client with otel_enabled=True
    49→        # so the background tracing thread knows to use OTel-only mode.
    50→        from langsmith.client import Client
    51→        from langsmith.run_trees import configure
    52→
    53→        configure(client=Client(otel_enabled=True))
    54→
    55→        # Enable auto-instrumentation for Claude Agent SDK
    56→        from langsmith.integrations.claude_agent_sdk import configure_claude_agent_sdk
    57→
    58→        if configure_claude_agent_sdk():
    59→            logger.info("Claude Agent SDK auto-instrumentation enabled")
    60→        else:
    61→            logger.warning(
    62→                "Failed to configure Claude Agent SDK auto-instrumentation"
    63→            )
    64→
    65→    except Exception as e:

    67→        _langfuse_client = None
    68→
    69→
    70→def get_langfuse_client():
    71→    """Return the Langfuse client or None if not configured."""
    72→    return _langfuse_client
    73→
    74→
    75→def get_langfuse_dashboard_url() -> str | None:
    76→    """Return the Langfuse project traces URL or None."""
    77→    if _langfuse_client is None:
    78→        return None
    79→    try:
    80→        project_id = _langfuse_client._get_project_id()
    81→        if project_id:
    82→            return f"{LANGFUSE_BASE_URL}/project/{project_id}/traces"
    83→    except Exception as e:
    84→        logger.warning("Failed to get Langfuse project ID: %s", e)
    85→    return LANGFUSE_BASE_URL
